import dotenv from 'dotenv';
import express, { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { createServer as createViteServer } from 'vite';
import {
  createArticle,
  createComment,
  deleteArticle,
  getArticleById,
  getSettingJson,
  likeArticle,
  listAllComments,
  listArticles,
  listCommentsForArticle,
  renameArticleTypeInArticles,
  saveSettingJson,
  subscribeNewsletter,
  voteComment,
} from './src/db/newsRepository.ts';
import { getOrCreateUser } from './src/db/users.ts';
import {
  AdminSidebarMenuItem,
  ArticleTypeOption,
  CustomShareChannel,
  DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES,
  DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS,
  MenuConfig,
  normalizeAdminSidebarMenus,
  normalizeMenuConfig,
  normalizeShareChannels,
} from './src/types/news.ts';

dotenv.config();

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const ADMIN_TOKEN = 'islam_admin_secret_token_2026';

interface AdminRequest extends Request {
  isAdmin?: boolean;
}

const requireAdmin = (req: AdminRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'دسترسی غیرمجاز. لطفاً از طریق پنل مدیریت وارد شوید.' });
  }
  const token = authHeader.split('Bearer ')[1];
  if (token !== ADMIN_TOKEN) {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'توکن مدیریت نامعتبر است.' });
  }
  req.isAdmin = true;
  next();
};

async function startServer() {
  const app = express();
  const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 3000;
  const UPLOADS_DIR = path.resolve(__dirname, 'uploads');
  fs.mkdirSync(UPLOADS_DIR, { recursive: true });

  app.use(express.json({ limit: '80mb' }));
  app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true, limit: '80mb' }));
  app.use('/uploads', express.static(UPLOADS_DIR));

  // Admin Login route
  app.post('/api/admin/login', (req, res) => {
    const { username, password } = req.body || {};
    if (username === 'admin' && password === 'islamtimes1405') {
      return res.json({ token: ADMIN_TOKEN, username: 'admin' });
    }
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است.' });
  });

  // Menu Configuration API
  app.get('/api/menus', async (_req, res) => {
    try {
      const saved = await getSettingJson<MenuConfig>('menu_config');
      res.json({ menuConfig: normalizeMenuConfig(saved) });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت تنظیمات منو';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.put('/api/menus', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const rawConfig = req.body?.menuConfig as MenuConfig | undefined;
      if (!rawConfig || !Array.isArray(rawConfig.items)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'ساختار تنظیمات منوها نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const normalized = normalizeMenuConfig(rawConfig);
      const saved = await saveSettingJson<MenuConfig>('menu_config', normalized);
      res.json({ menuConfig: saved });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ذخیره تنظیمات منو';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  // Article Types Configuration API
  app.get('/api/article-types', async (_req, res) => {
    try {
      const saved = await getSettingJson<ArticleTypeOption[]>('article_types');
      const types =
        Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length > 0 ? saved : DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES;
      res.json({ articleTypes: types });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت انواع مطلب';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.put('/api/article-types', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = req.body?.articleTypes as ArticleTypeOption[] | undefined;
      if (!Array.isArray(incoming) || incoming.length === 0) {
        return res
          .status(400)
          .json({ error: 'حداقل یک نوع مطلب باید در سامانه وجود داشته باشد.' });
      }
      const cleaned = incoming
        .map((t, idx) => ({
          id: String(t.id || `type-${Date.now()}-${idx}`),
          name: String(t.name || '').trim(),
          code: String(t.code || `type-${idx + 1}`).trim(),
          description: String(t.description || '').trim(),
          isDefault: Boolean(t.isDefault),
        }))
        .filter((t) => t.name.length > 0);

      if (cleaned.length === 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'عنوان نوع مطلب نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.' });
      }
      if (!cleaned.some((t) => t.isDefault)) {
        cleaned[0].isDefault = true;
      }

      const renamedFrom =
        typeof req.body?.renamedFrom === 'string' ? req.body.renamedFrom.trim() : '';
      const renamedTo =
        typeof req.body?.renamedTo === 'string' ? req.body.renamedTo.trim() : '';
      if (renamedFrom && renamedTo && renamedFrom !== renamedTo) {
        await renameArticleTypeInArticles(renamedFrom, renamedTo);
      }

      const saved = await saveSettingJson<ArticleTypeOption[]>('article_types', cleaned);
      res.json({ articleTypes: saved });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ذخیره انواع مطلب';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  // Admin Panel Sidebar Menu Configuration API
  app.get('/api/admin/sidebar-menus', async (_req, res) => {
    try {
      const saved = await getSettingJson<AdminSidebarMenuItem[]>('admin_sidebar_menus');
      res.json({ sidebarMenus: normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(saved) });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت منوهای پنل مدیریت';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.put('/api/admin/sidebar-menus', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = req.body?.sidebarMenus;
      if (!Array.isArray(incoming) || incoming.length === 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'ساختار منوهای پنل مدیریت نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const normalized = normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(incoming);
      const saved = await saveSettingJson<AdminSidebarMenuItem[]>(
        'admin_sidebar_menus',
        normalized
      );
      res.json({ sidebarMenus: saved });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ذخیره منوهای پنل مدیریت';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  // Custom Social Share Channels API
  app.get('/api/share-channels', async (_req, res) => {
    try {
      const saved = await getSettingJson<CustomShareChannel[]>('custom_share_channels');
      res.json({ channels: normalizeShareChannels(saved) });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت شبکه‌های اشتراک‌گذاری';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.put('/api/share-channels', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = req.body?.channels;
      if (!Array.isArray(incoming)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'ساختار فهرست شبکه‌ها نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const normalized = normalizeShareChannels(incoming);
      const saved = await saveSettingJson<CustomShareChannel[]>(
        'custom_share_channels',
        normalized
      );
      res.json({ channels: saved });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ذخیره شبکه‌های اشتراک‌گذاری';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/admin/comments', requireAdmin, async (_req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const allComments = await listAllComments();
      res.json({ comments: allComments });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت نظرات';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/news', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const section = typeof req.query.section === 'string' ? req.query.section : undefined;
      const search = typeof req.query.search === 'string' ? req.query.search : undefined;
      const language = typeof req.query.language === 'string' ? req.query.language : 'fa';
      const items = await listArticles({ section, search, language });
      res.json({ articles: items });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت اخبار';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/news/:id', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = Number(req.params.id);
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'شناسه خبر نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const article = await getArticleById(id);
      if (!article) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'خبر مورد نظر یافت نشد.' });
      }
      const articleComments = await listCommentsForArticle(id);
      res.json({ article, comments: articleComments });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در دریافت جزئیات خبر';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/news/:id/like', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = Number(req.params.id);
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'شناسه خبر نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const updated = await likeArticle(id);
      if (!updated) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'خبر یافت نشد.' });
      }
      res.json({ article: updated });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ثبت واکنش';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/comments/:id/vote', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = Number(req.params.id);
      const type = req.body?.type === 'dislike' ? 'dislike' : 'like';
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'شناسه نظر نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const updated = await voteComment(id, type);
      if (!updated) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'نظر یافت نشد.' });
      }
      res.json({ comment: updated });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ثبت رأی';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/newsletter', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const email = typeof req.body?.email === 'string' ? req.body.email.trim() : '';
      if (!email || !email.includes('@')) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'لطفاً یک نشانی ایمیل معتبر وارد کنید.' });
      }
      const result = await subscribeNewsletter(email, 'fa');
      res.status(201).json({ subscriber: result, message: 'عضویت شما در خبرنامه اسلام تایمز با موفقیت ثبت شد.' });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در عضویت خبرنامه';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/comments', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const authorName =
        typeof req.body?.authorName === 'string' && req.body.authorName.trim()
          ? req.body.authorName.trim()
          : 'کاربر اسلام تایمز';
      const authorEmail =
        typeof req.body?.authorEmail === 'string' && req.body.authorEmail.trim()
          ? req.body.authorEmail.trim()
          : 'user@example.com';
      const content = typeof req.body?.content === 'string' ? req.body.content.trim() : '';
      const showEmail = Boolean(req.body?.showEmail);
      const articleId = Number(req.body?.articleId);
      const parentId = req.body?.parentId ? Number(req.body.parentId) : null;

      if (!Number.isInteger(articleId) || articleId <= 0 || !content) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'متن نظر و شناسه خبر الزامی است.' });
      }

      // Ensure a visitor user record exists in DB
      const dbUser = await getOrCreateUser(
        'visitor-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(7),
        authorEmail,
        authorName,
        null
      );

      const created = await createComment({
        articleId,
        userId: dbUser.id,
        parentId,
        authorName,
        authorEmail,
        showEmail,
        content,
      });

      res.status(201).json({ comment: created });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ارسال نظر';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  // Protected Admin Media & File Upload
  app.post('/api/admin/upload', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const originalName =
        typeof req.body?.filename === 'string' && req.body.filename.trim()
          ? req.body.filename.trim()
          : 'file.bin';
      const mimeType =
        typeof req.body?.mimeType === 'string' && req.body.mimeType.trim()
          ? req.body.mimeType.trim()
          : 'application/octet-stream';
      const rawBase64 = typeof req.body?.dataBase64 === 'string' ? req.body.dataBase64 : '';
      const folderRaw = typeof req.body?.folder === 'string' ? req.body.folder : 'general';
      const allowedFolders = new Set(['images', 'attachments', 'gallery', 'videos', 'general']);
      const safeFolder = allowedFolders.has(folderRaw) ? folderRaw : 'general';

      if (!rawBase64) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'محتوای فایل برای آپلود ارسال نشده است.' });
      }

      const cleanBase64 = rawBase64.includes(';base64,')
        ? rawBase64.split(';base64,')[1]
        : rawBase64;
      const buffer = Buffer.from(cleanBase64, 'base64');
      if (buffer.length === 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'فایل انتخاب‌شده خالی یا نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      if (buffer.length > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'حداکثر حجم مجاز هر فایل ۵۰ مگابایت است.' });
      }

      const targetDir = path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, safeFolder);
      fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });

      const ext = path.extname(originalName).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9.]/g, '').slice(0, 12) || '';
      const storedName = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}${ext}`;
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(targetDir, storedName), buffer);

      res.status(201).json({
        url: `/uploads/${safeFolder}/${storedName}`,
        name: originalName,
        size: buffer.length,
        mimeType,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در آپلود فایل';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  // Protected Admin News Publishing
  app.post('/api/news', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const title = typeof req.body?.title === 'string' ? req.body.title.trim() : '';
      const lead = typeof req.body?.lead === 'string' ? req.body.lead.trim() : '';
      const body = typeof req.body?.body === 'string' ? req.body.body.trim() : '';
      const section = typeof req.body?.section === 'string' ? req.body.section.trim() : 'asia';
      const subsection =
        typeof req.body?.subsection === 'string' && req.body.subsection.trim()
          ? req.body.subsection.trim()
          : 'ایران';
      const imageUrl =
        typeof req.body?.imageUrl === 'string' && req.body.imageUrl.trim()
          ? req.body.imageUrl.trim()
          : '/src/assets/images/tehran_skyline_news_1794648748695.jpg';
      const tagsRaw = Array.isArray(req.body?.tags) ? req.body.tags : ['ایران', 'خبر فوری'];
      const attachmentsRaw = Array.isArray(req.body?.attachments) ? req.body.attachments : [];
      const galleryRaw = Array.isArray(req.body?.galleryImages) ? req.body.galleryImages : [];
      const videosRaw = Array.isArray(req.body?.videos) ? req.body.videos : [];

      if (!title || !lead || !body) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'عنوان، لید و متن خبر الزامی است.' });
      }

      const authorName =
        typeof req.body?.authorName === 'string' && req.body.authorName.trim()
          ? req.body.authorName.trim()
          : 'مدیر تحریریه';
      const savedTypes = await getSettingJson<ArticleTypeOption[]>('article_types');
      const activeTypes =
        Array.isArray(savedTypes) && savedTypes.length > 0
          ? savedTypes
          : DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES;
      const defaultTypeName =
        activeTypes.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.name || activeTypes[0]?.name || 'خبر';
      const rawType =
        typeof req.body?.articleType === 'string' ? req.body.articleType.trim() : '';
      const articleType = activeTypes.some((t) => t.name === rawType)
        ? rawType
        : defaultTypeName;

      const attachments = attachmentsRaw
        .filter((item: unknown) => item && typeof item === 'object' && (item as { url?: unknown }).url)
        .map((item: { id?: unknown; name?: unknown; url?: unknown; size?: unknown; mimeType?: unknown }, idx: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id || `att-${Date.now()}-${idx}`),
          name: String(item.name || `فایل ضمیمه ${idx + 1}`),
          url: String(item.url),
          size: typeof item.size === 'number' ? item.size : undefined,
          mimeType: typeof item.mimeType === 'string' ? item.mimeType : undefined,
        }));

      const galleryImages = galleryRaw
        .filter((item: unknown) => item && typeof item === 'object' && (item as { url?: unknown }).url)
        .map((item: { id?: unknown; url?: unknown; caption?: unknown }, idx: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id || `gal-${Date.now()}-${idx}`),
          url: String(item.url),
          caption: typeof item.caption === 'string' ? item.caption : '',
        }));

      const videos = videosRaw
        .filter((item: unknown) => item && typeof item === 'object' && (item as { url?: unknown }).url)
        .map((item: { id?: unknown; url?: unknown; title?: unknown; mimeType?: unknown }, idx: number) => ({
          id: String(item.id || `vid-${Date.now()}-${idx}`),
          url: String(item.url),
          title: typeof item.title === 'string' ? item.title : '',
          mimeType: typeof item.mimeType === 'string' ? item.mimeType : 'video/mp4',
        }));

      const article = await createArticle({
        section,
        subsection,
        title,
        lead,
        body,
        imageUrl,
        authorName,
        authorAvatar: '/src/assets/images/journalist_avatar_1794648750931.jpg',
        articleType,
        isVideo: articleType === 'ویدیو' || articleType === 'ویدئو' || videos.length > 0,
        tags: tagsRaw.map((t: unknown) => String(t)),
        attachments,
        galleryImages,
        videos,
      });

      res.status(201).json({ article });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در ثبت خبر';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/news/:id', requireAdmin, async (req: AdminRequest, res) => {
    try {
      const id = Number(req.params.id);
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'شناسه خبر نامعتبر است.' });
      }
      const deleted = await deleteArticle(id);
      if (!deleted) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'خبر مورد نظر یافت نشد.' });
      }
      res.json({ deletedId: id });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'خطا در حذف خبر';
      res.status(500).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    app.use('/src/assets', express.static(path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets')));
    app.use(express.static(path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist')));
    app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist', 'index.html'));
    });
  }

  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`Islam Times server running on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Failed to start server:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle, RefreshCw, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { AdminPage } from './components/AdminPage';
import { ArticleDetailView } from './components/ArticleDetailView';
import { CategoryView } from './components/CategoryView';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';
import { Header } from './components/Header';
import { HomeView } from './components/HomeView';
import { VideoPlayerModal } from './components/VideoPlayerModal';
import { AdminAuthProvider } from './context/AdminAuthContext';
import {
  Article,
  ArticleTypeOption,
  CommentItem,
  CustomShareChannel,
  DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES,
  DEFAULT_MENU_CONFIG,
  DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS,
  MenuConfig,
  normalizeMenuConfig,
  normalizeShareChannels,
  SectionKey,
} from './types/news';

function IslamTimesPortal() {
  const [articles, setArticles] = useState<Article[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<SectionKey>('home');
  const [activeSubsection, setActiveSubsection] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeArticleType, setActiveArticleType] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedArticle, setSelectedArticle] = useState<Article | null>(null);
  const [articleComments, setArticleComments] = useState<CommentItem[]>([]);

  const [menuConfig, setMenuConfig] = useState<MenuConfig>(DEFAULT_MENU_CONFIG);
  const [articleTypes, setArticleTypes] = useState<ArticleTypeOption[]>(DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES);
  const [shareChannels, setShareChannels] = useState<CustomShareChannel[]>(DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS);
  const [playingVideoArticle, setPlayingVideoArticle] = useState<Article | null>(
    null
  );
  const [langNotice, setLangNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const isAdminRoute =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
    (window.location.pathname === '/admin' ||
      window.location.pathname.startsWith('/admin/') ||
      new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('admin') === '1');

  const loadArticles = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/news?language=fa');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در بارگذاری اخبار');
      }
      const list: Article[] = Array.isArray(data.articles) ? data.articles : [];
      setArticles(list);

      const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      const codeParam = params.get('code');
      if (codeParam) {
        const matched = list.find((a) => a.code === codeParam);
        if (matched) {
          openArticleDetail(matched);
        }
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ارتباط با پایگاه داده برقرار نشد.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadMenuConfig = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/menus');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.menuConfig && Array.isArray(data.menuConfig.items)) {
        setMenuConfig(normalizeMenuConfig(data.menuConfig));
      }
    } catch {
      // keep default menu config on transient failure
    }
  };

  const loadArticleTypes = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/article-types');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && Array.isArray(data.articleTypes) && data.articleTypes.length > 0) {
        setArticleTypes(data.articleTypes);
      }
    } catch {
      // keep default article types on transient failure
    }
  };

  const loadShareChannels = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/share-channels');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && Array.isArray(data.channels)) {
        setShareChannels(normalizeShareChannels(data.channels));
      }
    } catch {
      // keep default channels
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadArticles();
    loadMenuConfig();
    loadArticleTypes();
    loadShareChannels();

    let bc: BroadcastChannel | null = null;
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined') {
      bc = new BroadcastChannel('islamtimes_cms_sync');
      bc.onmessage = (ev) => {
        if (ev.data?.type === 'MENU_UPDATED' && ev.data.menuConfig) {
          setMenuConfig(normalizeMenuConfig(ev.data.menuConfig));
        } else if (ev.data?.type === 'ARTICLE_TYPES_UPDATED' && Array.isArray(ev.data.articleTypes)) {
          setArticleTypes(ev.data.articleTypes);
          loadArticles();
        } else if (ev.data?.type === 'SHARE_CHANNELS_UPDATED' && Array.isArray(ev.data.channels)) {
          setShareChannels(normalizeShareChannels(ev.data.channels));
        } else if (ev.data?.type === 'ARTICLES_UPDATED') {
          loadArticles();
        }
      };
    }

    return () => {
      if (bc) bc.close();
    };
  }, []);

  const openArticleDetail = async (article: Article) => {
    setSelectedArticle(article);
    setArticleComments([]);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/news/${article.id}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.article) {
        setSelectedArticle(data.article);
        setArticleComments(Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : []);
        setArticles((prev) =>
          prev.map((a) => (a.id === data.article.id ? data.article : a))
        );
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep local article data visible even if view counter request fails
    }
  };

  const handleSelectSection = (section: SectionKey, subsection: string | null = null) => {
    setSelectedArticle(null);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setActiveArticleType(null);
    setActiveSection(section);
    setActiveSubsection(subsection);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const handleSelectArticleTypeArchive = (typeName: string) => {
    setSelectedArticle(null);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setActiveSection('home');
    setActiveSubsection(null);
    setActiveArticleType(typeName);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const handleSearchSubmit = (q: string) => {
    setSelectedArticle(null);
    setActiveSubsection(null);
    setActiveArticleType(null);
    setSearchQuery(q.trim());
    if (q.trim()) {
      setActiveSection('west-asia');
    } else {
      setActiveSection('home');
    }
  };

  const handleTagClick = (tag: string) => {
    setSelectedArticle(null);
    setActiveSubsection(null);
    setActiveArticleType(null);
    setSearchQuery(tag);
    setActiveSection('west-asia');
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const filteredArticles = useMemo(() => {
    return articles.filter((item) => {
      if (searchQuery) {
        const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
        const hay = `${item.title} ${item.lead} ${item.body} ${item.subsection} ${item.tags} ${item.articleType || ''}`.toLowerCase();
        return hay.includes(q);
      }
      if (activeArticleType) {
        const itemType = item.articleType || (item.isVideo ? 'ویدیو' : 'خبر');
        const matchesType =
          itemType === activeArticleType ||
          (activeArticleType === 'ویدیو' && item.isVideo) ||
          (activeArticleType === 'گفتگو' && item.section === 'interview');
        if (!matchesType) return false;
      }
      if (activeSection === 'home') return true;
      if (activeSubsection) {
        const sub = activeSubsection.toLowerCase();
        if (item.section === activeSection) {
          return (
            item.subsection.toLowerCase() === sub ||
            item.title.toLowerCase().includes(sub) ||
            item.lead.toLowerCase().includes(sub) ||
            item.tags.toLowerCase().includes(sub)
          );
        }
        return (
          item.subsection.toLowerCase() === sub ||
          item.tags.toLowerCase().includes(sub)
        );
      }
      return item.section === activeSection;
    });
  }, [articles, activeSection, activeSubsection, activeArticleType, searchQuery]);

  const handleUnavailableLang = (langLabel: string) => {
    setLangNotice(
      `نسخه فارسی هم‌اکنون فعال است. نسخه زبان «${langLabel}» پس از دستور بعدی شما راه‌اندازی خواهد شد.`
    );
    setTimeout(() => {
      setLangNotice((prev) => (prev?.includes(langLabel) ? null : prev));
    }, 5000);
  };

  const notifySync = (payload: {
    type: string;
    menuConfig?: MenuConfig;
    articleTypes?: ArticleTypeOption[];
    channels?: CustomShareChannel[];
  }) => {
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined') {
      const bc = new BroadcastChannel('islamtimes_cms_sync');
      bc.postMessage(payload);
      bc.close();
    }
  };

  if (isAdminRoute) {
    return (
      <AdminPage
        articles={articles}
        menuConfig={menuConfig}
        articleTypes={articleTypes}
        shareChannels={shareChannels}
        onMenuConfigSaved={(next) => {
          setMenuConfig(next);
          notifySync({ type: 'MENU_UPDATED', menuConfig: next });
        }}
        onArticleTypesSaved={(nextTypes) => {
          setArticleTypes(nextTypes);
          loadArticles();
          notifySync({ type: 'ARTICLE_TYPES_UPDATED', articleTypes: nextTypes });
        }}
        onShareChannelsSaved={(nextChannels) => {
          setShareChannels(nextChannels);
          notifySync({ type: 'SHARE_CHANNELS_UPDATED', channels: nextChannels });
        }}
        onArticlePublished={(newArticle) => {
          setArticles((prev) => [newArticle, ...prev]);
          notifySync({ type: 'ARTICLES_UPDATED' });
        }}
        onArticleDeleted={(articleId) => {
          setArticles((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== articleId));
          notifySync({ type: 'ARTICLES_UPDATED' });
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  const isHomePage =
    activeSection === 'home' && !searchQuery && !selectedArticle && !activeArticleType;
  const currentMenuLabel = menuConfig.items.find((m) => m.key === activeSection)?.label;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-white text-neutral-900">
      <Header
        activeSection={selectedArticle ? (selectedArticle.section as SectionKey) : activeSection}
        activeSubsection={selectedArticle ? selectedArticle.subsection : activeSubsection}
        isHomePage={isHomePage}
        menuConfig={menuConfig}
        onSelectSection={handleSelectSection}
        searchQuery={searchQuery}
        onSearchSubmit={handleSearchSubmit}
        onSelectUnavailableLang={handleUnavailableLang}
      />

      {langNotice && (
        <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 mt-4 w-full">
          <div className="bg-sky-50 border border-sky-200 text-sky-900 rounded px-4 py-2.5 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <span>{langNotice}</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setLangNotice(null)}
              aria-label="بستن پیام"
              className="text-sky-700 hover:text-sky-950 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 mt-6 w-full">
          <div className="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-[#d41a24] rounded p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={loadArticles}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded bg-[#d41a24] text-white font-semibold cursor-pointer shrink-0"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              تلاش مجدد
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex-1">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 space-y-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
              <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-4">
                {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
                  <div key={`load-r-${i}`} className="h-16 bg-neutral-100 animate-pulse rounded" />
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="lg:col-span-6 h-80 bg-neutral-100 animate-pulse rounded" />
              <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-4">
                {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
                  <div key={`load-l-${i}`} className="h-16 bg-neutral-100 animate-pulse rounded" />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : selectedArticle ? (
          <ArticleDetailView
            article={selectedArticle}
            comments={articleComments}
            allArticles={articles}
            menuConfig={menuConfig}
            shareChannels={shareChannels}
            onSelectArticle={openArticleDetail}
            onSelectSection={handleSelectSection}
            onSelectArticleType={handleSelectArticleTypeArchive}
            onArticleUpdated={(updated) => {
              setSelectedArticle(updated);
              setArticles((prev) =>
                prev.map((a) => (a.id === updated.id ? updated : a))
              );
            }}
            onCommentAdded={(newComment) =>
              setArticleComments((prev) => [newComment, ...prev])
            }
            onCommentUpdated={(updatedComment) =>
              setArticleComments((prev) =>
                prev.map((c) => (c.id === updatedComment.id ? updatedComment : c))
              )
            }
            onTagClick={handleTagClick}
          />
        ) : activeSection === 'home' && !searchQuery && !activeArticleType ? (
          <HomeView
            articles={articles}
            menuConfig={menuConfig}
            onSelectArticle={openArticleDetail}
            onSelectSection={handleSelectSection}
            onSelectArticleType={handleSelectArticleTypeArchive}
            onPlayVideoArticle={(vid) => setPlayingVideoArticle(vid)}
          />
        ) : (
          <CategoryView
            section={activeSection}
            sectionLabelOverride={currentMenuLabel}
            activeSubsection={activeSubsection}
            activeArticleType={activeArticleType}
            articleTypes={articleTypes}
            categoryMediaMode={menuConfig.categoryMediaMode || 'both'}
            searchQuery={searchQuery}
            articles={filteredArticles}
            allArticles={articles}
            onSelectArticle={openArticleDetail}
            onSelectArticleType={(typeName) => setActiveArticleType(typeName)}
            onPlayVideoArticle={(vid) => setPlayingVideoArticle(vid)}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <Footer
        menuConfig={menuConfig}
        articleTypes={articleTypes}
        onSelectSection={handleSelectSection}
        onSelectArticleType={handleSelectArticleTypeArchive}
        onSearchSubmit={handleSearchSubmit}
        onSelectUnavailableLang={handleUnavailableLang}
      />

      <VideoPlayerModal
        article={playingVideoArticle}
        onClose={() => setPlayingVideoArticle(null)}
        onReadFullReport={openArticleDetail}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AdminAuthProvider>
      <IslamTimesPortal />
    </AdminAuthProvider>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Loader2, Lock, User, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminAuth } from '../context/AdminAuthContext';

interface AdminLoginModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

export function AdminLoginModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
}: AdminLoginModalProps) {
  const { adminLogin, loginError } = useAdminAuth();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('admin');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('islamtimes1405');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [localErr, setLocalErr] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalErr(null);
    if (!username.trim() || !password.trim()) {
      setLocalErr('لطفاً نام کاربری و رمز عبور را وارد کنید.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    const ok = await adminLogin(username.trim(), password.trim());
    setLoading(false);
    if (ok) {
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="bg-white rounded-md shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden border border-neutral-200">
        <div className="bg-[#0b5e85] text-white px-5 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-200" />
            <h2 className="text-sm md:text-base font-bold">ورود به پنل مدیریت اسلام تایمز</h2>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="بستن"
            className="text-white/80 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-neutral-50 rounded p-3 text-xs text-neutral-600 border border-neutral-200">
            <p className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">اطلاعات پیش‌فرض مدیریت:</p>
            <p>نام کاربری: <code className="bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-300 font-mono">admin</code></p>
            <p className="mt-1">رمز عبور: <code className="bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-300 font-mono">islamtimes1405</code></p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
              نام کاربری
            </label>
            <div className="relative flex items-center">
              <User className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute right-3" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0b5e85]"
                placeholder="نام کاربری..."
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
              رمز عبور
            </label>
            <div className="relative flex items-center">
              <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute right-3" />
              <input
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0b5e85]"
                placeholder="رمز عبور..."
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {(localErr || loginError) && (
            <p className="text-xs text-[#d41a24] font-medium">
              {localErr || loginError}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded bg-neutral-200 hover:bg-neutral-300 text-neutral-800 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
            >
              انصراف
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 rounded bg-[#0b5e85] hover:bg-[#084867] text-white text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-60 transition-colors"
            >
              {loading && <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />}
              ورود به مدیریت
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  onArticleTypesSaved: (next: ArticleTypeOption[]) => void;

}

type AdminTab = AdminSidebarMenuId;

const ADMIN_TAB_ICONS: Record<AdminSidebarMenuId, typeof BarChart3> = {
  overview: BarChart3,
  menus: Menu,
  articles: Newspaper,
  editorial: UserCheck,
  reporters: Users,
  comments: MessageSquare,
};

export function AdminPage({
  articles,
  menuConfig,
  articleTypes,
  shareChannels,
  onMenuConfigSaved,
  onArticleTypesSaved,
  onShareChannelsSaved,
  onArticlePublished,
  onArticleDeleted,
}: AdminPageProps) {
  const { isAdmin, adminLogin, adminLogout, getAdminToken, loginError } = useAdminAuth();

  // Full-Page Login State
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('admin');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('islamtimes1405');
  const [loginLoading, setLoginLoading] = useState(false);
  const [localLoginErr, setLocalLoginErr] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // CMS Tab State & Customizable Admin Sidebar
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AdminTab>('menus');
  const [menuSubTab, setMenuSubTab] = useState<
    'structure' | 'home-layout' | 'types' | 'share-channels'
  >('structure');
  const [highlightedBlockId, setHighlightedBlockId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sidebarMenus, setSidebarMenus] = useState<AdminSidebarMenuItem[]>(
    DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS
  );
  const [editingSidebar, setEditingSidebar] = useState(false);
  const [savingSidebar, setSavingSidebar] = useState(false);
  const [sidebarStatus, setSidebarStatus] = useState<{
    type: 'success' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Menu Management State
  const [draftMenu, setDraftMenu] = useState<MenuConfig>(normalizeMenuConfig(menuConfig));
  const [savingMenu, setSavingMenu] = useState(false);
  const [menuStatus, setMenuStatus] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [newMainLabel, setNewMainLabel] = useState('');
  const [newMainKey, setNewMainKey] = useState('');
  const [newSubByMenuId, setNewSubByMenuId] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [editingMainId, setEditingMainId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editingMainValue, setEditingMainValue] = useState('');
  const [editingSubKey, setEditingSubKey] = useState<{ menuId: string; subId: string } | null>(null);
  const [editingSubValue, setEditingSubValue] = useState('');

  // Article Types Management State
  const [draftTypes, setDraftTypes] = useState<ArticleTypeOption[]>(
    articleTypes.length > 0 ? articleTypes : DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES
  );
  const [savingTypes, setSavingTypes] = useState(false);
  const [typesStatus, setTypesStatus] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [newTypeName, setNewTypeName] = useState('');
  const [newTypeDesc, setNewTypeDesc] = useState('');
  const [editingTypeId, setEditingTypeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editingTypeName, setEditingTypeName] = useState('');
  const [editingTypeDesc, setEditingTypeDesc] = useState('');

  // Share Channels Management State (Custom / Other Networks)
  const [draftChannels, setDraftChannels] = useState<CustomShareChannel[]>(
    shareChannels && shareChannels.length > 0
      ? shareChannels
      : DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS
  );
  const [savingChannels, setSavingChannels] = useState(false);
  const [channelsStatus, setChannelsStatus] = useState<{
    type: 'success' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [newChannelName, setNewChannelName] = useState('');
  const [newChannelUrl, setNewChannelUrl] = useState('');
  const [newChannelColor, setNewChannelColor] = useState('#0b5e85');
  const [editingChannelId, setEditingChannelId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editChannelName, setEditChannelName] = useState('');
  const [editChannelUrl, setEditChannelUrl] = useState('');
  const [editChannelColor, setEditChannelColor] = useState('#0b5e85');

  // Other CMS States
  const [editorialList] = useState<EditorialMember[]>(INITIAL_EDITORIAL);
  const [reportersList, setReportersList] = useState<ReporterItem[]>(INITIAL_REPORTERS);
  const [allComments, setAllComments] = useState<CommentItem[]>([]);

  const initialMenuSection =
    draftMenu.items.find((m) => m.key !== 'home') || draftMenu.items[0];
  const defaultTypeName =
    draftTypes.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.name || draftTypes[0]?.name || 'خبر';

  // Article Publish Form State
  const [showAddArticle, setShowAddArticle] = useState(false);
  const [articleTypeFilter, setArticleTypeFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [articleSection, setArticleSection] = useState<string>(
    initialMenuSection?.key || 'west-asia'
  );
  const [articleSubsection, setArticleSubsection] = useState<string>(
    initialMenuSection?.subsections[0]?.label || 'لبنان'
  );
  const [articleType, setArticleType] = useState<string>(defaultTypeName);
  const [articleTitle, setArticleTitle] = useState('');
  const [articleLead, setArticleLead] = useState('');
  const [articleBody, setArticleBody] = useState('');
  const [articleImageUrl, setArticleImageUrl] = useState(NEWSROOM_IMAGES[0].url);
  const [articleAttachments, setArticleAttachments] = useState<ArticleAttachment[]>([]);
  const [articleGalleryImages, setArticleGalleryImages] = useState<ArticleGalleryItem[]>([]);
  const [articleVideos, setArticleVideos] = useState<ArticleVideoItem[]>([]);
  const [newGalleryUrl, setNewGalleryUrl] = useState('');
  const [newVideoUrl, setNewVideoUrl] = useState('');
  const [uploadingMainImage, setUploadingMainImage] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingAttachment, setUploadingAttachment] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingGallery, setUploadingGallery] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingVideo, setUploadingVideo] = useState(false);
  const [articleAuthor, setArticleAuthor] = useState('دکتر محمدرضا صادقی');
  const [articleTags, setArticleTags] = useState('غرب آسیا، اسلام تایمز');
  const [submittingArticle, setSubmittingArticle] = useState(false);
  const [articleError, setArticleError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [deletingArticleId, setDeletingArticleId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  // Staff forms
  const [newStaffName, setNewStaffName] = useState('');
  const [newStaffRole, setNewStaffRole] = useState('خبرنگار ارشد');
  const [newStaffLoc, setNewStaffLoc] = useState('تهران');

  useEffect(() => {
    setDraftMenu(normalizeMenuConfig(menuConfig));
  }, [menuConfig]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (articleTypes.length > 0) {
      setDraftTypes(articleTypes);
    }
  }, [articleTypes]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (shareChannels && shareChannels.length > 0) {
      setDraftChannels(shareChannels);
    }
  }, [shareChannels]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (draftTypes.length > 0 && !draftTypes.some((t) => t.name === articleType)) {
      setArticleType(draftTypes.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.name || draftTypes[0].name);
    }
  }, [draftTypes, articleType]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/admin/sidebar-menus')
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (Array.isArray(data.sidebarMenus)) {
          setSidebarMenus(normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(data.sidebarMenus));
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Keep default sidebar menus on transient error
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) return;
    fetch('/api/admin/comments', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (Array.isArray(data.comments)) {
          setAllComments(data.comments);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // ignore transient error
      });
  }, [isAdmin, getAdminToken]);

  const persistSidebarMenus = async (nextMenus: AdminSidebarMenuItem[]) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      setSidebarStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: 'نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است.',
      });
      return;
    }
    setSavingSidebar(true);
    setSidebarStatus(null);
    try {
      const cleaned = normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(nextMenus);
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/sidebar-menus', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ sidebarMenus: cleaned }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در ذخیره منوهای پنل');
      }
      setSidebarMenus(normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(data.sidebarMenus));
      setSidebarStatus({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'نام و ترتیب منوهای پنل ذخیره شد.',
      });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setSidebarStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ذخیره ناموفق بود.',
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingSidebar(false);
    }
  };

  const handleMoveSidebarMenu = (index: number, direction: -1 | 1) => {
    const target = index + direction;
    if (target < 0 || target >= sidebarMenus.length) return;
    const next = [...sidebarMenus];
    [next[index], next[target]] = [next[target], next[index]];
    setSidebarMenus(next);
    persistSidebarMenus(next);
  };

  const handleSidebarLabelInput = (id: AdminSidebarMenuId, label: string) => {
    setSidebarMenus((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, label } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleResetSidebarMenus = () => {
    setSidebarMenus(DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS);
    persistSidebarMenus(DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS);
  };

  const handleLoginSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalLoginErr(null);
    if (!username.trim() || !password.trim()) {
      setLocalLoginErr('لطفاً نام کاربری و رمز عبور را وارد کنید.');
      return;
    }
    setLoginLoading(true);
    await adminLogin(username.trim(), password.trim());
    setLoginLoading(false);
  };

  const persistMenuConfig = async (nextConfig: MenuConfig) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      setMenuStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است. لطفاً دوباره وارد شوید.' });
      return;
    }
    setSavingMenu(true);
    setMenuStatus(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/menus', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ menuConfig: nextConfig }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در ذخیره تنظیمات منو');
      }
      onMenuConfigSaved(data.menuConfig);
      setMenuStatus({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'تنظیمات منوها با موفقیت در پایگاه داده ذخیره و در سایت اعمال شد.',
      });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setMenuStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ذخیره تنظیمات منوها ناموفق بود.',
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingMenu(false);
    }
  };

  const persistArticleTypes = async (
    nextTypes: ArticleTypeOption[],
    renameInfo?: { from: string; to: string }
  ) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      setTypesStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: 'نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است. لطفاً دوباره وارد شوید.',
      });
      return;
    }
    setSavingTypes(true);
    setTypesStatus(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/article-types', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          articleTypes: nextTypes,
          renamedFrom: renameInfo?.from,
          renamedTo: renameInfo?.to,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در ذخیره انواع مطلب');
      }

      const savedTypes: ArticleTypeOption[] = Array.isArray(data.articleTypes)
        ? data.articleTypes
        : nextTypes;
      setDraftTypes(savedTypes);
      onArticleTypesSaved(savedTypes);

      if (renameInfo && renameInfo.from !== renameInfo.to) {
        if (articleType === renameInfo.from) {
          setArticleType(renameInfo.to);
        }
        if (articleTypeFilter === renameInfo.from) {
          setArticleTypeFilter(renameInfo.to);
        }
        const blocks = draftMenu.homeBlocks || DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS;
        const hasAffectedBlock = blocks.some(
          (b) => b.sourceType === 'article-type' && b.sectionKey === renameInfo.from
        );
        if (hasAffectedBlock) {
          const updatedMenu: MenuConfig = {
            ...draftMenu,
            homeBlocks: blocks.map((b) =>
              b.sourceType === 'article-type' && b.sectionKey === renameInfo.from
                ? {
                    ...b,
                    sectionKey: renameInfo.to,
                    title: b.title === renameInfo.from ? renameInfo.to : b.title,
                  }
                : b
            ),
          };
          setDraftMenu(updatedMenu);
          await persistMenuConfig(updatedMenu);
        }
      }

      setTypesStatus({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'تغییرات انواع مطلب ذخیره شد و در تمام بخش‌های مدیریت و سایت فعال گردید.',
      });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setTypesStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ذخیره انواع مطلب ناموفق بود.',
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingTypes(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddArticleType = () => {
    const cleanName = newTypeName.trim();
    if (!cleanName) {
      setTypesStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'لطفاً عنوان نوع مطلب را وارد کنید.' });
      return;
    }
    if (draftTypes.some((t) => t.name === cleanName)) {
      setTypesStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'این نوع مطلب قبلاً ثبت شده است.' });
      return;
    }
    const newItem: ArticleTypeOption = {
      id: `type-${Date.now()}`,
      name: cleanName,
      code: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
      description: newTypeDesc.trim() || `مطالب و گزارش‌های مرتبط با ${cleanName}`,
      isDefault: draftTypes.length === 0,
    };
    const next = [...draftTypes, newItem];
    setDraftTypes(next);
    setNewTypeName('');
    setNewTypeDesc('');
    persistArticleTypes(next);
  };

  const handleStartEditType = (t: ArticleTypeOption) => {
    setEditingTypeId(t.id);
    setEditingTypeName(t.name);
    setEditingTypeDesc(t.description || '');
    setTypesStatus(null);
  };

  const handleSaveEditType = (typeId: string) => {
    const cleanName = editingTypeName.trim();
    if (!cleanName) {
      setTypesStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'نام نوع مطلب نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.' });
      return;
    }
    if (draftTypes.some((t) => t.id !== typeId && t.name === cleanName)) {
      setTypesStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'نوع مطلب دیگری با این نام وجود دارد.' });
      return;
    }
    const existing = draftTypes.find((t) => t.id === typeId);
    if (!existing) return;

    const oldName = existing.name;
    const next = draftTypes.map((t) =>
      t.id === typeId
        ? {
            ...t,
            name: cleanName,
            description: editingTypeDesc.trim() || t.description,
          }
        : t
    );
    setDraftTypes(next);
    setEditingTypeId(null);
    setEditingTypeName('');
    setEditingTypeDesc('');
    persistArticleTypes(
      next,
      oldName !== cleanName ? { from: oldName, to: cleanName } : undefined
    );
  };

  const handleDeleteArticleType = (typeId: string) => {
    if (draftTypes.length <= 1) {
      setTypesStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: 'حداقل یک نوع مطلب باید در سامانه باقی بماند.',
      });
      return;
    }
    const target = draftTypes.find((t) => t.id === typeId);
    const filtered = draftTypes.filter((t) => t.id !== typeId);
    const hasDefault = filtered.some((t) => t.isDefault);
    const next = hasDefault
      ? filtered
      : filtered.map((t, idx) => ({ ...t, isDefault: idx === 0 }));
    if (target && articleType === target.name) {
      setArticleType(next.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.name || next[0].name);
    }
    if (target && articleTypeFilter === target.name) {
      setArticleTypeFilter('all');
    }
    setDraftTypes(next);
    persistArticleTypes(next);
  };

  const handleSetDefaultArticleType = (typeId: string) => {
    const next = draftTypes.map((t) => ({
      ...t,
      isDefault: t.id === typeId,
    }));
    setDraftTypes(next);
    persistArticleTypes(next);
  };

  const handleMoveArticleType = (index: number, direction: -1 | 1) => {
    const target = index + direction;
    if (target < 0 || target >= draftTypes.length) return;
    const next = [...draftTypes];
    const [moved] = next.splice(index, 1);
    next.splice(target, 0, moved);
    setDraftTypes(next);
    persistArticleTypes(next);
  };

  const handleResetDefaultArticleTypes = () => {
    setDraftTypes(DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES);
    setEditingTypeId(null);
    persistArticleTypes(DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES);
  };

  const persistShareChannels = async (nextList: CustomShareChannel[]) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      setChannelsStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: 'نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است. لطفاً دوباره وارد شوید.',
      });
      return;
    }
    setSavingChannels(true);
    setChannelsStatus(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/share-channels', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ channels: nextList }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در ذخیره شبکه‌ها');
      }
      const saved = normalizeShareChannels(data.channels);
      setDraftChannels(saved);
      onShareChannelsSaved?.(saved);
      setChannelsStatus({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'تنظیمات شبکه‌های اشتراک‌گذاری با موفقیت در پایگاه داده ذخیره شد.',
      });
      setTimeout(() => setChannelsStatus(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setChannelsStatus({
        type: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ذخیره شبکه‌ها ناموفق بود.',
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingChannels(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddShareChannel = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newChannelName.trim()) {
      setChannelsStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'لطفاً نام شبکه اجتماعی را وارد کنید.' });
      return;
    }
    if (!newChannelUrl.trim()) {
      setChannelsStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'لطفاً آدرس لینک اشتراک‌گذاری را وارد کنید.' });
      return;
    }
    const newCh: CustomShareChannel = {
      id: `custom-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      name: newChannelName.trim(),
      urlTemplate: newChannelUrl.trim(),
      color: newChannelColor.trim() || '#0b5e85',
      enabled: true,
    };
    const nextList = [...draftChannels, newCh];
    setDraftChannels(nextList);
    setNewChannelName('');
    setNewChannelUrl('');
    setNewChannelColor('#0b5e85');
    persistShareChannels(nextList);
  };

  const handleDeleteShareChannel = (id: string) => {
    const nextList = draftChannels.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
    setDraftChannels(nextList);
    persistShareChannels(nextList);
  };

  const handleToggleShareChannel = (id: string) => {
    const nextList = draftChannels.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, enabled: !c.enabled } : c));
    setDraftChannels(nextList);
    persistShareChannels(nextList);
  };

  const handleMoveShareChannel = (id: string, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const idx = draftChannels.findIndex((c) => c.id === id);
    const targetIdx = idx + dir;
    if (idx < 0 || targetIdx < 0 || targetIdx >= draftChannels.length) return;
    const copy = [...draftChannels];
    [copy[idx], copy[targetIdx]] = [copy[targetIdx], copy[idx]];
    setDraftChannels(copy);
    persistShareChannels(copy);
  };

  const handleSaveEditShareChannel = (id: string) => {
    if (!editChannelName.trim() || !editChannelUrl.trim()) return;
    const nextList = draftChannels.map((c) =>
      c.id === id
        ? {
            ...c,
            name: editChannelName.trim(),
            urlTemplate: editChannelUrl.trim(),
            color: editChannelColor.trim() || '#0b5e85',
          }
        : c
    );
    setDraftChannels(nextList);
    setEditingChannelId(null);
    persistShareChannels(nextList);
  };

  const handleResetDefaultShareChannels = () => {
    setDraftChannels(DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS);
    setEditingChannelId(null);
    persistShareChannels(DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS);
  };

  const handleAddMainMenu = () => {
    const label = newMainLabel.trim();
    if (!label) return;
    const rawKey = newMainKey.trim() || `custom-${Date.now()}`;
    const cleanKey = rawKey.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
    if (draftMenu.items.some((m) => m.key === cleanKey)) {
      setMenuStatus({ type: 'error', text: 'کلید یکتای این منو تکراری است.' });
      return;
    }
    const newItem: MainMenuItemConfig = {
      id: `menu-${Date.now()}`,
      key: cleanKey,
      label,
      showOnHome: true,
      showOnMenuPage: true,
      homeSubmenuLimit: 14,
      menuPageSubmenuLimit: 14,
      subsections: [],
    };
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      maxMainMenusOnHome: Math.max(draftMenu.maxMainMenusOnHome, draftMenu.items.length + 1),
      maxMainMenusOnMenuPage: Math.max(draftMenu.maxMainMenusOnMenuPage, draftMenu.items.length + 1),
      items: [...draftMenu.items, newItem],
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    setNewMainLabel('');
    setNewMainKey('');
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleDeleteMainMenu = (menuId: string) => {
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.filter((m) => m.id !== menuId),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleMoveMainMenu = (index: number, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const target = index + dir;
    if (target < 0 || target >= draftMenu.items.length) return;
    const copy = [...draftMenu.items];
    [copy[index], copy[target]] = [copy[target], copy[index]];
    const next = { ...draftMenu, items: copy };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleSaveMainMenuName = (menuId: string) => {
    const trimmed = editingMainValue.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) =>
        m.id === menuId ? { ...m, label: trimmed } : m
      ),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    setEditingMainId(null);
    setEditingMainValue('');
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleSaveSubmenuName = (menuId: string, subId: string) => {
    const trimmed = editingSubValue.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) =>
        m.id === menuId
          ? {
              ...m,
              subsections: m.subsections.map((s) =>
                s.id === subId ? { ...s, label: trimmed } : s
              ),
            }
          : m
      ),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    setEditingSubKey(null);
    setEditingSubValue('');
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleAddSubmenu = (menuId: string) => {
    const label = (newSubByMenuId[menuId] || '').trim();
    if (!label) return;
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) => {
        if (m.id !== menuId) return m;
        if (m.subsections.some((s) => s.label === label)) return m;
        return {
          ...m,
          subsections: [
            ...m.subsections,
            {
              id: `${m.key}-sub-${Date.now()}`,
              label,
              showOnHome: true,
              showOnMenuPage: true,
            },
          ],
        };
      }),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    setNewSubByMenuId((prev) => ({ ...prev, [menuId]: '' }));
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleDeleteSubmenu = (menuId: string, subId: string) => {
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) =>
        m.id === menuId
          ? { ...m, subsections: m.subsections.filter((s) => s.id !== subId) }
          : m
      ),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleMoveSubmenu = (menuId: string, subIdx: number, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) => {
        if (m.id !== menuId) return m;
        const target = subIdx + dir;
        if (target < 0 || target >= m.subsections.length) return m;
        const copy = [...m.subsections];
        [copy[subIdx], copy[target]] = [copy[target], copy[subIdx]];
        return { ...m, subsections: copy };
      }),
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const handleQuickAddToHomeLayout = (
    sourceType: 'menu' | 'submenu',
    sectionKey: string,
    title: string,
    subsectionLabel?: string
  ) => {
    const currentBlocks = draftMenu.homeBlocks || DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS;
    const existing = currentBlocks.find((b) =>
      sourceType === 'submenu'
        ? b.sourceType === 'submenu' &&
          b.sectionKey === sectionKey &&
          b.subsectionLabel === subsectionLabel
        : b.sourceType === 'menu' && b.sectionKey === sectionKey
    );

    if (existing) {
      setHighlightedBlockId(existing.id);
      setMenuSubTab('home-layout');
      return;
    }

    const newBlock: HomeLayoutBlock = {
      id: `block-${Date.now()}`,
      enabled: true,
      title,
      sourceType,
      sectionKey,
      subsectionLabel: subsectionLabel || null,
      zone: 'main',
      columnSpan: 'half',
      layout: 'lead-with-list',
      articleCount: 4,
      showNumbers: false,
      strictFilter: sourceType === 'submenu',
    };

    const next: MenuConfig = {
      ...draftMenu,
      homeBlocks: [...currentBlocks, newBlock],
    };
    setDraftMenu(next);
    setHighlightedBlockId(newBlock.id);
    setMenuSubTab('home-layout');
    persistMenuConfig(next);
  };

  const readFileAsDataUrl = (file: File): Promise<string> =>
    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
      reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('خواندن فایل انتخاب‌شده ناموفق بود.'));
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });

  const uploadAdminFile = async (
    file: File,
    folder: 'images' | 'attachments' | 'gallery' | 'videos'
  ) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      throw new Error('نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است.');
    }
    const dataBase64 = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
    const res = await fetch('/api/admin/upload', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        filename: file.name,
        mimeType: file.type,
        folder,
        dataBase64,
      }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در آپلود فایل');
    }
    return data as { url: string; name: string; size: number; mimeType: string };
  };

  const formatFileSize = (bytes?: number) => {
    if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '';
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${toPersianDigits(bytes)} بایت`;
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${toPersianDigits(Math.round(bytes / 1024))} کیلوبایت`;
    return `${toPersianDigits((bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1))} مگابایت`;
  };

  const handleMainImageUpload = async (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
    setArticleError(null);
    setUploadingMainImage(true);
    try {
      const uploaded = await uploadAdminFile(files[0], 'images');
      setArticleImageUrl(uploaded.url);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setArticleError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'آپلود تصویر اصلی ناموفق بود.');
    } finally {
      setUploadingMainImage(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAttachmentsUpload = async (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
    setArticleError(null);
    setUploadingAttachment(true);
    try {
      const uploadedList: ArticleAttachment[] = [];
      for (const file of Array.from(files)) {
        const uploaded = await uploadAdminFile(file, 'attachments');
        uploadedList.push({
          id: `att-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
          name: uploaded.name,
          url: uploaded.url,
          size: uploaded.size,
          mimeType: uploaded.mimeType,
        });
      }
      setArticleAttachments((prev) => [...prev, ...uploadedList]);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setArticleError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'آپلود فایل ضمیمه ناموفق بود.');
    } finally {
      setUploadingAttachment(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGalleryUpload = async (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
    setArticleError(null);
    setUploadingGallery(true);
    try {
      const uploadedList: ArticleGalleryItem[] = [];
      for (const file of Array.from(files)) {
        const uploaded = await uploadAdminFile(file, 'gallery');
        uploadedList.push({
          id: `gal-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
          url: uploaded.url,
          caption: '',
        });
      }
      setArticleGalleryImages((prev) => [...prev, ...uploadedList]);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setArticleError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'آپلود تصویر گالری ناموفق بود.');
    } finally {
      setUploadingGallery(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVideosUpload = async (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
    setArticleError(null);
    setUploadingVideo(true);
    try {
      const uploadedList: ArticleVideoItem[] = [];
      for (const file of Array.from(files)) {
        const uploaded = await uploadAdminFile(file, 'videos');
        uploadedList.push({
          id: `vid-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
          url: uploaded.url,
          title: file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''),
          mimeType: uploaded.mimeType || 'video/mp4',
        });
      }
      setArticleVideos((prev) => [...prev, ...uploadedList]);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setArticleError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'آپلود ویدیو ناموفق بود.');
    } finally {
      setUploadingVideo(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePublishArticle = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setArticleError(null);
    if (!articleTitle.trim() || !articleLead.trim() || !articleBody.trim()) {
      setArticleError('لطفاً عنوان، لید و متن خبر را تکمیل کنید.');
      return;
    }

    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) {
      setArticleError('نشست مدیریت منقضی شده است.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingArticle(true);
    try {
      const tags = articleTags
        .split(/[،,]/)
        .map((t) => t.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      const res = await fetch('/api/news', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          section: articleSection,
          subsection: articleSubsection,
          articleType,
          title: articleTitle.trim(),
          lead: articleLead.trim(),
          body: articleBody.trim().startsWith('به گزارش اسلام تایمز:')
            ? articleBody.trim()
            : `به گزارش اسلام تایمز: ${articleBody.trim()}`,
          imageUrl: articleImageUrl,
          tags,
          authorName: articleAuthor,
          attachments: articleAttachments,
          galleryImages: articleGalleryImages,
          videos: articleVideos,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در ثبت خبر');
      onArticlePublished(data.article);
      setArticleTitle('');
      setArticleLead('');
      setArticleBody('');
      setArticleImageUrl(NEWSROOM_IMAGES[0].url);
      setArticleAttachments([]);
      setArticleGalleryImages([]);
      setArticleVideos([]);
      setNewGalleryUrl('');
      setNewVideoUrl('');
      setArticleType(defaultTypeName);
      setShowAddArticle(false);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setArticleError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ثبت خبر ناموفق بود.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingArticle(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteArticle = async (articleId: number) => {
    const token = getAdminToken();
    if (!token) return;
    setDeletingArticleId(articleId);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/news/${articleId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        onArticleDeleted(articleId);
      }
    } finally {
      setDeletingArticleId(null);
    }
  };

  // Unauthenticated full-page login view (NOT a modal)
  if (!isAdmin) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-100 flex flex-col justify-between" dir="rtl">
        <div className="bg-[#0b5e85] text-white px-6 py-4 flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <IslamTimesLogo compact />
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-sky-100 border-r border-white/25 pr-3">
              سامانه مدیریت محتوا (CMS)
            </span>
          </div>
          <a
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 px-3 py-1.5 rounded font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            بازگشت به صفحه اصلی سایت
          </a>
        </div>

        <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-neutral-200 w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-[#0b5e85] text-white px-6 py-4 flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-200" />
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-base font-bold">ورود به پنل مدیریت اسلام تایمز</h1>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-sky-100">صفحه اختصاصی مدیریت محتوا، منوها و تحریریه</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-neutral-50 rounded p-3 text-xs text-neutral-600 border border-neutral-200">
                <p className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 mb-1">اطلاعات ورود مدیر:</p>
                <p>
                  نام کاربری:{' '}
                  <code className="bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-300 font-mono">
                    admin
                  </code>
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1">
                  رمز عبور:{' '}
                  <code className="bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-300 font-mono">
                    islamtimes1405
                  </code>
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  نام کاربری
                </label>
                <div className="relative flex items-center">
                  <User className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute right-3" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0b5e85]"
                    placeholder="نام کاربری..."
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  رمز عبور
                </label>
                <div className="relative flex items-center">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute right-3" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 pr-9 pl-3 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0b5e85]"
                    placeholder="رمز عبور..."
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {(localLoginErr || loginError) && (
                <p className="text-xs text-[#d41a24] font-medium">
                  {localLoginErr || loginError}
                </p>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loginLoading}
                className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded bg-[#0b5e85] hover:bg-[#084867] text-white text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-60 transition-colors"
              >
                {loginLoading && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />}
                ورود به سامانه مدیریت CMS
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </main>

        <footer className="py-3 text-center text-[11px] text-neutral-500 border-t border-neutral-200 bg-white">
          سامانه مدیریت محتوای پایگاه خبری اسلام تایمز
        </footer>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const sectionMenusForPublish = draftMenu.items.filter((m) => m.key !== 'home');
  const selectedMenuForPublish = sectionMenusForPublish.find((m) => m.key === articleSection);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-100 flex flex-col" dir="rtl">
      {/* Top Admin Bar */}
      <header className="bg-[#0b5e85] text-white px-6 py-3.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-md sticky top-0 z-30">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <IslamTimesLogo compact />
          <div className="border-r border-white/25 pr-3">
            <h1 className="text-sm font-bold">پنل جامع مدیریت محتوا (CMS) — اسلام تایمز</h1>
