                                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                                        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                                          e.preventDefault();
                                          handleSaveSubmenuName(menuItem.id, sub.id);
                                        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                                          setEditingSubKey(null);
                                        }
                                      }}
                                      autoFocus
                                      className="flex-1 min-w-0 rounded border border-[#0b5e85] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-900 bg-white focus:outline-none"
                                    />
                                    <button
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() => handleSaveSubmenuName(menuItem.id, sub.id)}
                                      className="p-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-600 text-white cursor-pointer"
                                      title="ذخیره"
                                    >
                                      <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
                                    </button>
                                    <button
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() => setEditingSubKey(null)}
                                      className="p-0.5 rounded bg-neutral-200 text-neutral-700 cursor-pointer"
                                      title="انصراف"
                                    >
                                      <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                                    </button>
                                  </div>
                                ) : (
                                  <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800 truncate">
                                    {sub.label}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </div>

                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => {
                                    setEditingSubKey({ menuId: menuItem.id, subId: sub.id });
                                    setEditingSubValue(sub.label);
                                  }}
                                  className="p-0.5 text-neutral-400 hover:text-[#0b5e85] cursor-pointer"
                                  title="تغییر نام زیرمنو"
                                >
                                  <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    setDraftMenu({
                                      ...draftMenu,
                                      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) =>
                                        m.id === menuItem.id
                                          ? {
                                              ...m,
                                              subsections: m.subsections.map((s) =>
                                                s.id === sub.id
                                                  ? { ...s, showOnHome: !s.showOnHome }
                                                  : s
                                              ),
                                            }
                                          : m
                                      ),
                                    })
                                  }
                                  className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                                    sub.showOnHome
                                      ? 'bg-sky-100 text-[#0b5e85]'
                                      : 'bg-neutral-200 text-neutral-500 line-through'
                                  }`}
                                  title="نمایش در صفحه اصلی"
                                >
                                  اصلی
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    setDraftMenu({
                                      ...draftMenu,
                                      items: draftMenu.items.map((m) =>
                                        m.id === menuItem.id
                                          ? {
                                              ...m,
                                              subsections: m.subsections.map((s) =>
                                                s.id === sub.id
                                                  ? { ...s, showOnMenuPage: !s.showOnMenuPage }
                                                  : s
                                              ),
                                            }
                                          : m
                                      ),
                                    })
                                  }
                                  className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                                    sub.showOnMenuPage
                                      ? 'bg-rose-100 text-[#d41a24]'
                                      : 'bg-neutral-200 text-neutral-500 line-through'
                                  }`}
                                  title="نمایش در صفحه منو"
                                >
                                  منو
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    handleQuickAddToHomeLayout(
                                      'submenu',
                                      menuItem.key,
                                      sub.label,
                                      sub.label
                                    )
                                  }
                                  className="p-0.5 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded cursor-pointer"
                                  title="نمایش و چینش در صفحه اول"
                                >
                                  <LayoutGrid className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleDeleteSubmenu(menuItem.id, sub.id)}
                                  className="p-0.5 text-neutral-400 hover:text-[#d41a24] cursor-pointer"
                                  title="حذف زیرمنو"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: OVERVIEW */}
          {activeTab === 'overview' && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">کل مقالات و اخبار</p>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#0b5e85] mt-1">
                      {toPersianDigits(articles.length)}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-[#0b5e85]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[#0b5e85]">
                    <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">منوهای اصلی فعال</p>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#d41a24] mt-1">
                      {toPersianDigits(draftMenu.items.length)}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-[#d41a24]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[#d41a24]">
                    <Layers className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">خبرنگاران و نویسندگان</p>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-600 mt-1">
                      {toPersianDigits(reportersList.length)}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-600">
                    <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-neutral-500 text-xs font-medium">مجموع نظرات کاربران</p>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600 mt-1">
                      {toPersianDigits(allComments.length)}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-amber-600">
                    <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 space-y-4">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 border-b border-neutral-100 pb-3">
                  دسترسی سریع به بخش‌های مدیریت
                </h3>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab('menus')}
                    className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-[#0b5e85]/5 hover:bg-[#0b5e85]/10 border border-[#0b5e85]/20 text-right cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[#0b5e85] font-bold text-xs mb-1">
                      <Menu className="w-4 h-4" />
                      مدیریت منوها و زیرمنوها
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                      حذف و اضافه منوها و تنظیم تعداد نمایش در صفحات
                    </p>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setActiveTab('articles');
                      setShowAddArticle(true);
                    }}
                    className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 border border-rose-200 text-right cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[#d41a24] font-bold text-xs mb-1">
                      <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                      انتشار خبر جدید
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                      انتشار فوری خبر در بخش‌ها و زیربخش‌های سایت
                    </p>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab('reporters')}
                    className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 border border-neutral-200 text-right cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-neutral-900 font-bold text-xs mb-1">
                      <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
                      مدیریت خبرنگاران
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                      افزودن و مدیریت خبرنگاران دفاتر منطقه‌ای
                    </p>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB: ARTICLES MANAGEMENT */}
          {activeTab === 'articles' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">مدیریت اخبار و مقالات</h2>
                  <p className="text-neutral-500 text-xs">
                    فهرست کامل اخبار منتشرشده در پایگاه داده
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 font-semibold">فیلتر نوع مطلب:</span>
                    <select
                      value={articleTypeFilter}
                      onChange={(e) => setArticleTypeFilter(e.target.value)}
                      className="rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800"
                    >
                      <option value="all">همه انواع ({toPersianDigits(articles.length)})</option>
                      {draftTypes.map((t) => (
                        <option key={t.id} value={t.name}>
                          {t.name}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowAddArticle(true)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded bg-[#d41a24] hover:bg-[#b5141d] text-white text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    انتشار خبر جدید
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {showAddArticle && (
                <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 shadow-sm space-y-4">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0b5e85] border-b border-neutral-100 pb-2">
                    فرم انتشار خبر جدید
                  </h3>
                  <form onSubmit={handlePublishArticle} className="space-y-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                          نوع مطلب
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={articleType}
                          onChange={(e) => setArticleType(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white font-semibold"
                        >
                          {draftTypes.map((t) => (
                            <option key={t.id} value={t.name}>
                              {t.name}
                              {t.isDefault ? ' (پیش‌فرض)' : ''}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                          منوی اصلی / سرویس خبری
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={articleSection}
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            const nextSec = e.target.value;
                            setArticleSection(nextSec);
                            const matched = draftMenu.items.find((m) => m.key === nextSec);
                            setArticleSubsection(
                              matched?.subsections[0]?.label || matched?.label || nextSec
                            );
                          }}
                          className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white"
                        >
                          {sectionMenusForPublish.map((m) => (
                            <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>
                              {m.label}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                          زیرمنو / زیربخش
                        </label>
                        {selectedMenuForPublish && selectedMenuForPublish.subsections.length > 0 ? (
                          <select
                            value={articleSubsection}
                            onChange={(e) => setArticleSubsection(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white"
                          >
                            {selectedMenuForPublish.subsections.map((sub) => (
                              <option key={sub.id} value={sub.label}>
                                {sub.label}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        ) : (
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={articleSubsection}
                            onChange={(e) => setArticleSubsection(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5"
                          />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                          نویسنده / خبرنگار
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={articleAuthor}
                          onChange={(e) => setArticleAuthor(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white"
                        >
                          <option value="دکتر محمدرضا صادقی">دکتر محمدرضا صادقی (سردبیر)</option>
                          {reportersList.map((r) => (
                            <option key={r.id} value={r.name}>
                              {r.name} ({r.location})
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        تیتر خبر
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={articleTitle}
                        onChange={(e) => setArticleTitle(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-1.5"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        لید (خلاصه خبر)
                      </label>
                      <textarea
                        rows={2}
                        value={articleLead}
                        onChange={(e) => setArticleLead(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 p-2"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        متن کامل خبر
                      </label>
                      <textarea
                        rows={4}
                        value={articleBody}
                        onChange={(e) => setArticleBody(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 p-2"
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* Main Photo: Upload + Preset + Direct URL */}
                    <div className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-md p-3.5 space-y-3">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <label className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0b5e85]" />
                          تصویر اصلی خبر (آپلود از سیستم یا انتخاب تصویر پیش‌فرض)
                        </label>
                        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#0b5e85] hover:bg-[#084867] text-white text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer">
                          {uploadingMainImage ? (
                            <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                          ) : (
                            <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          )}
                          <span>{uploadingMainImage ? 'در حال آپلود...' : 'آپلود عکس خبر'}</span>
                          <input
                            type="file"
                            accept="image/*"
                            disabled={uploadingMainImage}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              handleMainImageUpload(e.target.files);
                              e.target.value = '';
                            }}
                            className="hidden"
                          />
                        </label>
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-[120px_1fr_1fr] gap-3 items-center">
                        <div className="w-full h-20 rounded border border-neutral-300 overflow-hidden bg-white flex items-center justify-center">
                          <img
                            src={articleImageUrl}
                            alt="پیش‌نمایش تصویر خبر"
                            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                            انتخاب از آرشیو تحریریه
                          </label>
                          <select
                            value={
                              NEWSROOM_IMAGES.some((i) => i.url === articleImageUrl)
                                ? articleImageUrl
                                : ''
                            }
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              if (e.target.value) setArticleImageUrl(e.target.value);
                            }}
                            className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs"
                          >
                            <option value="">-- تصویر آپلودشده / دلخواه --</option>
                            {NEWSROOM_IMAGES.map((img) => (
                              <option key={img.url} value={img.url}>
                                {img.label}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                            نشانی مستقیم تصویر (خودکار پس از آپلود پر می‌شود)
                          </label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            dir="ltr"
                            value={articleImageUrl}
                            onChange={(e) => setArticleImageUrl(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs font-mono"
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Photo Gallery Section */}
                    <div className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-md p-3.5 space-y-3">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <label className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0b5e85]" />
                          گالری عکس ({toPersianDigits(articleGalleryImages.length)} تصویر)
                        </label>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              dir="ltr"
                              value={newGalleryUrl}
                              onChange={(e) => setNewGalleryUrl(e.target.value)}
                              placeholder="https://... نشانی عکس"
                              className="w-44 rounded border border-neutral-300 bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px]"
                            />
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => {
                                const url = newGalleryUrl.trim();
                                if (!url) return;
                                setArticleGalleryImages((prev) => [
                                  ...prev,
                                  { id: `gal-${Date.now()}`, url, caption: '' },
                                ]);
                                setNewGalleryUrl('');
                              }}
                              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded border border-neutral-300 bg-white hover:bg-neutral-100 text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                              افزودن لینک
                            </button>
                          </div>
                          <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer">
                            {uploadingGallery ? (
                              <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                            ) : (
                              <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            )}
                            <span>{uploadingGallery ? 'در حال آپلود...' : 'آپلود تصاویر گالری'}</span>
                            <input
                              type="file"

                              multiple

                              }}

                            />
                          </label>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                              <img
                                src={img.url}

                                className="w-16 h-12 rounded object-cover shrink-0 border border-neutral-200"
                              />
                              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                                <input
                                  type="text"

                            </div>
                          ))}

                                    title: `ویدیو ${toPersianDigits(prev.length + 1)}`,
                                    mimeType: 'video/mp4',

                                ]);
                                setNewVideoUrl('');

                              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded border border-neutral-300 bg-white hover:bg-neutral-100 text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer"

                                  className="flex-1 rounded border border-neutral-200 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold"
                                />
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    setArticleVideos((prev) =>
                                      prev.filter((item) => item.id !== vid.id)
                                    )

                                  className="p-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-[#d41a24] cursor-pointer"
                                  title="حذف ویدیو"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              </div>
                              <video
                                src={vid.url}
                                controls

                                className="w-full max-h-36 rounded bg-black"

                            </div>

                      <button

                        انصراف

                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
