import { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, ExternalLink, Instagram, Lock, LogOut, MessageCircle, Search, Send, Share2, Youtube } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAdminAuth } from '../context/AdminAuthContext';
import { LANGUAGES, MainMenuItemConfig, MenuConfig, SectionKey } from '../types/news';
import { IslamTimesLogo } from './IslamTimesLogo';

interface HeaderProps {
  activeSection: SectionKey;
  activeSubsection?: string | null;
  isHomePage: boolean;
  menuConfig: MenuConfig;
  onSelectSection: (section: SectionKey, subsection?: string | null) => void;
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchSubmit: (q: string) => void;
  onSelectUnavailableLang: (langLabel: string) => void;
}

export function Header({
  activeSection,
  activeSubsection,
  isHomePage,
  menuConfig,
  onSelectSection,
  searchQuery,
  onSearchSubmit,
  onSelectUnavailableLang,
}: HeaderProps) {
  const [localSearch, setLocalSearch] = useState(searchQuery);
  const { isAdmin, adminLogout } = useAdminAuth();

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    onSearchSubmit(localSearch);
  };

  const showMainMenu = isHomePage
    ? menuConfig.showMainMenuOnHome
    : menuConfig.showMainMenuOnMenuPage;
  const maxMainMenus = isHomePage
    ? menuConfig.maxMainMenusOnHome
    : menuConfig.maxMainMenusOnMenuPage;
  const showSubmenus = isHomePage
    ? menuConfig.showSubmenusOnHome
    : menuConfig.showSubmenusOnMenuPage;
  const maxSubmenusGlobal = isHomePage
    ? menuConfig.maxSubmenusOnHome
    : menuConfig.maxSubmenusOnMenuPage;

  const visibleMainMenus = showMainMenu
    ? menuConfig.items
        .filter((m) => (isHomePage ? m.showOnHome : m.showOnMenuPage))
        .slice(0, maxMainMenus)
    : [];

  const getVisibleSubmenus = (item: MainMenuItemConfig) => {
    if (!showSubmenus) return [];
    const perMenuLimit = isHomePage ? item.homeSubmenuLimit : item.menuPageSubmenuLimit;
    const cap = Math.min(maxSubmenusGlobal, perMenuLimit > 0 ? perMenuLimit : maxSubmenusGlobal);
    return item.subsections
      .filter((s) => (isHomePage ? s.showOnHome : s.showOnMenuPage))
      .slice(0, cap);
  };

  const activeMenuItem = menuConfig.items.find((m) => m.key === activeSection);
  const activeBarSubmenus = activeMenuItem ? getVisibleSubmenus(activeMenuItem) : [];

  return (
    <header className="w-full no-print">
      {/* Top Language & Date Bar */}
      <div className="w-full bg-[#0b5e85] text-white text-xs border-b border-white/15">
        <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 py-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="text-sky-100 tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
            ۱۴ فروردین ۱۴۰۵ / April 9 2026
          </div>

          <div
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap justify-center"
            role="navigation"
            aria-label="انتخاب زبان پایگاه خبری"
          >
            {LANGUAGES.map((lang) => (
              <button
                key={lang.code}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (!lang.active) {
                    onSelectUnavailableLang(lang.label);
                  }
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  lang.active
                    ? 'bg-[#d41a24] text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'bg-[#1982b0] text-white/95 hover:bg-[#2394c4]'
                }`}
              >
                {lang.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            {isAdmin ? (
              <>
                <a
                  href="/admin"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/30 text-white text-[11px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 transition-colors"
                >
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  ورود به پنل مدیریت CMS
                </a>
                <span className="text-sky-100 text-[11px] px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-black/20 rounded">
                  مدیر سیستم
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={adminLogout}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-black/30 hover:bg-black/50 text-white text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                  title="خروج از مدیریت"
                >
                  <LogOut className="w-3 h-3" />
                  خروج
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <a
                href="/admin"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white text-[11px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 transition-colors"
              >
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                پنل مدیریت
                <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 opacity-80" />
              </a>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Blue Brand Banner */}
      <div className="w-full islam-times-banner-bg pt-4 pb-8">
        <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <IslamTimesLogo
            onClick={() => {
              setLocalSearch('');
              onSearchSubmit('');
              onSelectSection('home');
            }}
          />

          <form
            onSubmit={handleSubmit}
            className="w-full max-w-md flex items-center bg-white rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden border border-white/60"
          >
            <input
              type="text"
              value={localSearch}
              onChange={(e) => setLocalSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="جستجو در اخبار اسلام تایمز..."
              className="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs md:text-sm text-neutral-800 placeholder:text-neutral-400 focus:outline-none bg-transparent"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              aria-label="جستجو"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-[#0b5e85] hover:text-[#d41a24] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 text-white">
            <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2.5 text-white/90">
              <a
                href="#footer-social"
                aria-label="اینستاگرام"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Instagram className="w-4 h-4" />
              </a>
              <a
                href="#footer-social"
                aria-label="تلگرام"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              </a>
              <a
                href="#footer-social"
                aria-label="یوتیوب"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Youtube className="w-4 h-4" />
              </a>
              <a
                href="#footer-social"
                aria-label="واتس‌اپ"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              </a>
              <a
                href="#footer-social"
                aria-label="اشتراک‌گذاری"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Floating White Section Navigation Bar */}
      {showMainMenu && visibleMainMenus.length > 0 && (
        <div className="max-w-[960px] mx-auto px-4 -mt-5 relative z-20">
          <nav
            className="bg-white rounded-md shadow-md border border-neutral-200/80 px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1 md:gap-1.5"
            aria-label="منوی اصلی بخش‌های خبری"
          >
            {visibleMainMenus.map((item, idx) => {
              const isActive = activeSection === item.key;
              const hasSub =
                item.subsections.some((s) =>
                  isHomePage ? s.showOnMenuPage || s.showOnHome : s.showOnMenuPage
                ) &&
                (isHomePage
                  ? menuConfig.showSubmenusOnMenuPage || menuConfig.showSubmenusOnHome
                  : menuConfig.showSubmenusOnMenuPage);
              return (
                <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center shrink-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectSection(item.key, null)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded text-xs md:text-[13px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'bg-[#d41a24] text-white'
                        : 'text-neutral-700 hover:text-[#d41a24]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                    {hasSub && (
                      <ChevronDown
                        className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 ${
                          isActive ? 'text-white/90' : 'text-neutral-400'
                        }`}
                      />
                    )}
                  </button>

                  {idx < visibleMainMenus.length - 1 && (
                    <span
                      className="mx-1 text-neutral-300 select-none text-xs"
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    >
                      |
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Active Section Submenu Strip (when on menu/category page) */}
          {activeBarSubmenus.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-2 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200/90 rounded-md px-3 py-2 flex items-center flex-wrap justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectSection(activeSection, null)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  !activeSubsection
                    ? 'bg-[#0b5e85] text-white'
                    : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-[#0b5e85] hover:bg-white'
                }`}
              >
                همه مطالب
              </button>
              {activeBarSubmenus.map((sub) => {
                const isSubActive = activeSubsection === sub.label;
                return (
                  <div key={`subbar-${sub.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="text-neutral-300 text-[10px] select-none" aria-hidden="true">
                      /
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onSelectSection(activeSection, sub.label)}
                      className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                        isSubActive
                          ? 'bg-[#d41a24] text-white font-semibold'
                          : 'text-neutral-700 hover:text-[#d41a24] hover:bg-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sub.label}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

  {
    key: 'full-bottom',
    label: 'نوار تمام‌عرض پایین صفحه (قبل از فوتر)',
    desc: 'بخش‌هایی که در پایین صفحه و در تمام عرض قبل از فوتر نمایش داده می‌شوند.',
    badgeColor: 'bg-emerald-700 text-white',
  },
];

const LAYOUT_OPTIONS: { key: HomeBlockLayout; label: string; hint: string }[] = [
  {
    key: 'featured-split',
    label: 'خبر بزرگ + لیست کناری',
    hint: 'یک خبر شاخص با عکس بزرگ و خلاصه + لیست اخبار کناری',
  },
  {
    key: 'lead-with-list',
    label: 'خبر شاخص بالا + لیست زیر آن',
    hint: 'یک تصویر بزرگ در بالا و بقیه مطالب به‌صورت فهرست در پایین',
  },
  {
    key: 'grid-2col',
    label: 'شبکه دو ستونه افقی',
    hint: 'نمایش کارت‌های تصویر و تیتر در دو ستون کنار هم',
  },
  {
    key: 'grid-4col',
    label: 'شبکه ۴ ستونه کارتی',
    hint: 'مناسب بخش‌های تمام‌عرض با ۴ کارت کنار هم',
  },
  {
    key: 'list-compact',
    label: 'لیست افقی فشرده',
    hint: 'فهرست عمودی کارت‌های تصویر کوچک و تیتر',
  },
  {
    key: 'headline-list',
    label: 'فهرست متنی تیترها با فلش',
    hint: 'لیست متنی و سریع تیتر اخبار (بدون تصویر)',
  },
  {
    key: 'avatar-list',
    label: 'لیست با تصویر دایره‌ای',
    hint: 'نمایش با آواتار گرد نویسنده/تصویر + تاریخ (مناسب گفت‌وگو)',
  },
  {
    key: 'media-dark',
    label: 'نوار تیره ویدیو / گزارش تصویری',
    hint: 'پس‌زمینه تیره برای نمایش شاخص ویدیوها یا گزارش‌های تصویری',
  },
];

export function HomeLayoutManager({
  menuConfig,
  articleTypes = DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES,
  onChangeMenuConfig,
  highlightedBlockId,
}: HomeLayoutManagerProps) {
  const activeTypes = articleTypes.length > 0 ? articleTypes : DEFAULT_ARTICLE_TYPES;
  const heroCfg: HomeHeroConfig = menuConfig.heroConfig || DEFAULT_HERO_CONFIG;
  const blocks: HomeLayoutBlock[] = menuConfig.homeBlocks || DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS;

  // Build list of available main menus directly from current MenuConfig items
  const menuOptions = menuConfig.items
    .filter((m) => m.key !== 'home')
    .map((m) => ({
      key: m.key,
      label: m.label,
      subsections: m.subsections.map((s) => s.label),
    }));

  // Add Block Builder State
  const firstMenuWithSubs =
    menuOptions.find((m) => m.subsections.length > 0) || menuOptions[0];

  const [newSourceType, setNewSourceType] = useState<HomeBlockSourceType>('menu');
  const [newSectionKey, setNewSectionKey] = useState<string>(
    firstMenuWithSubs?.key || 'west-asia'
  );
  const [newSubsectionLabel, setNewSubsectionLabel] = useState<string>(
    firstMenuWithSubs?.subsections[0] || ''
  );
  const [newSpecialFeedKey, setNewSpecialFeedKey] = useState<'most-viewed' | 'latest'>(
    'most-viewed'
  );
  const [newArticleTypeName, setNewArticleTypeName] = useState<string>(
    activeTypes[0]?.name || 'خبر'
  );
  const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState<string>('');
  const [newZone, setNewZone] = useState<HomeBlockZone>('main');
  const [newColSpan, setNewColSpan] = useState<'full' | 'half'>('half');
  const [newLayout, setNewLayout] = useState<HomeBlockLayout>('lead-with-list');
  const [newArticleCount, setNewArticleCount] = useState<number>(4);
  const [newStrictFilter, setNewStrictFilter] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [newShowNumbers, setNewShowNumbers] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [editingTitleBlockId, setEditingTitleBlockId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editingTitleValue, setEditingTitleValue] = useState<string>('');

  const selectedMenuForNew = menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === newSectionKey);

  const updateHero = (patch: Partial<HomeHeroConfig>) => {
    onChangeMenuConfig({
      ...menuConfig,
      heroConfig: { ...heroCfg, ...patch },
    });
  };

  const updateBlocks = (nextBlocks: HomeLayoutBlock[], autoPersist = false) => {
    onChangeMenuConfig(
      {
        ...menuConfig,
        homeBlocks: nextBlocks,
      },
      autoPersist
    );
  };

  const updateSingleBlock = (
    blockId: string,
    patch: Partial<HomeLayoutBlock>,
    autoPersist = false
  ) => {
    updateBlocks(
      blocks.map((b) => (b.id === blockId ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)),
      autoPersist
    );
  };

  const handleMoveInZone = (blockId: string, zone: HomeBlockZone, dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const zoneBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.zone === zone);
    const idxInZone = zoneBlocks.findIndex((b) => b.id === blockId);
    const targetIdxInZone = idxInZone + dir;
    if (idxInZone < 0 || targetIdxInZone < 0 || targetIdxInZone >= zoneBlocks.length) {
      return;
    }

    const swapTargetId = zoneBlocks[targetIdxInZone].id;
    const globalIdxA = blocks.findIndex((b) => b.id === blockId);
    const globalIdxB = blocks.findIndex((b) => b.id === swapTargetId);
    if (globalIdxA < 0 || globalIdxB < 0) return;

    const copy = [...blocks];
    [copy[globalIdxA], copy[globalIdxB]] = [copy[globalIdxB], copy[globalIdxA]];
    updateBlocks(copy, true);
  };

  const handleDeleteBlock = (blockId: string) => {
    updateBlocks(
      blocks.filter((b) => b.id !== blockId),
      true
    );
  };

  const handleAddBlock = () => {
    let resolvedSection = newSectionKey;
    let resolvedSub: string | null = null;
    let defaultTitle = '';

    if (newSourceType === 'special-feed') {
      resolvedSection = newSpecialFeedKey;
      defaultTitle =
        newSpecialFeedKey === 'most-viewed' ? 'پربیننده‌ترین' : 'آخرین عناوین';
    } else if (newSourceType === 'article-type') {
      resolvedSection = newArticleTypeName;
      defaultTitle = newArticleTypeName;
    } else if (newSourceType === 'submenu') {
      resolvedSub = newSubsectionLabel || selectedMenuForNew?.subsections[0] || '';
      defaultTitle =
        resolvedSub ||
        selectedMenuForNew?.label ||
        SECTION_LABELS[resolvedSection] ||
        resolvedSection;
    } else {
      defaultTitle =
        selectedMenuForNew?.label || SECTION_LABELS[resolvedSection] || resolvedSection;
    }

    const block: HomeLayoutBlock = {
      id: `block-${Date.now()}`,
      enabled: true,
      title: newTitle.trim() || defaultTitle,
      sourceType: newSourceType,
      sectionKey: resolvedSection,
      subsectionLabel: resolvedSub,
      zone: newZone,
      columnSpan: newZone === 'main' ? newColSpan : 'full',
      layout: newLayout,
      articleCount: Math.max(1, Math.min(30, newArticleCount || 4)),
      showNumbers: newShowNumbers,
      strictFilter: newStrictFilter,
    };

    updateBlocks([...blocks, block], true);
    setNewTitle('');
  };

  const resolveSourceBadge = (block: HomeLayoutBlock) => {
    if (block.sourceType === 'special-feed') {
      return block.sectionKey === 'most-viewed'
        ? 'خوراک ویژه: پربیننده‌ترین'
        : 'خوراک ویژه: آخرین عناوین';
    }
    if (block.sourceType === 'article-type') {
      return `نوع مطلب: ${block.sectionKey}`;
    }
    const menuLabel =
      menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === block.sectionKey)?.label ||
      SECTION_LABELS[block.sectionKey] ||
      block.sectionKey;
    if (block.sourceType === 'submenu' && block.subsectionLabel) {
      return `زیرمنو: ${menuLabel} ← ${block.subsectionLabel}`;
    }
    return `منوی اصلی: ${menuLabel}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 1. Top Hero Settings Card */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-100 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0b5e85]">
              تنظیمات بخش سرخط خبرهای بالای صفحه (Hero)
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
              کنترل نمایش، منبع منو/زیرمنو و تعداد اخبار ستون‌های راست و چپ در بالاترین بخش صفحه اول
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => updateHero({ enabled: !heroCfg.enabled })}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded border text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
              heroCfg.enabled
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700'
                : 'bg-neutral-100 border-neutral-300 text-neutral-500'
            }`}
          >
            {heroCfg.enabled ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            {heroCfg.enabled ? 'نمایش در صفحه اول فعال است' : 'بخش سرخط مخفی است'}
          </button>
        </div>

        {heroCfg.enabled && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                منبع سرخط اخبار
              </label>
              <select
                value={heroCfg.sourceType}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const nextType = e.target.value as 'all' | 'menu' | 'submenu';
                  const fallbackMenu = menuOptions[0]?.key || 'west-asia';
                  const fallbackSub =
                    menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === fallbackMenu)?.subsections[0] || '';
                  updateHero({
                    sourceType: nextType,
                    sectionKey: nextType === 'all' ? 'home' : fallbackMenu,
                    subsectionLabel: nextType === 'submenu' ? fallbackSub : null,
                  });
                }}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="all">همه اخبار سایت (خودکار)</option>
                <option value="menu">از یک منوی اصلی خاص</option>
                <option value="submenu">از یک زیرمنو (فرعی) خاص</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {heroCfg.sourceType !== 'all' && (
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                  انتخاب منوی اصلی
                </label>
                <select
                  value={heroCfg.sectionKey}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const sec = e.target.value;
                    const firstSub =
                      menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === sec)?.subsections[0] || '';
                    updateHero({
                      sectionKey: sec,
                      subsectionLabel: heroCfg.sourceType === 'submenu' ? firstSub : null,
                    });
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white"
                >
                  {menuOptions.map((m) => (
                    <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>
                      {m.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            )}

            {heroCfg.sourceType === 'submenu' && (
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                  انتخاب زیرمنو
                </label>
                <select
                  value={heroCfg.subsectionLabel || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => updateHero({ subsectionLabel: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white"
                >
                  {(
                    menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === heroCfg.sectionKey)?.subsections || []
                  ).map((sub) => (
                    <option key={sub} value={sub}>
                      {sub}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex items-end gap-3 md:col-span-2">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                  تعداد اخبار ستون راست سرخط
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={12}
                  value={heroCfg.sideRightCount}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    updateHero({
                      sideRightCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(12, Number(e.target.value) || 0)),
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white font-mono text-center"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                  تعداد اخبار ستون چپ سرخط
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={12}
                  value={heroCfg.sideLeftCount}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    updateHero({
                      sideLeftCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(12, Number(e.target.value) || 0)),
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white font-mono text-center"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 1.5. Category/Section Page Video & Photo Report Mode */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-3">
        <div className="border-b border-neutral-100 pb-2.5">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#0b5e85]">
            نمایش ویدیو و گزارش تصویری در صفحه بخش‌ها (منوها و زیرمنوها)
          </h3>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
            مشخص کنید در صفحات بخش‌ها و زیرمنوها، نوار «ویدیو»، نوار «گزارش تصویری» یا هر دو با هم نمایش داده شود.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5 text-xs">
          {(
            [
              {
                key: 'both',
                label: 'ویدیو و گزارش تصویری (هر دو با هم)',
                desc: 'نمایش هر دو بخش ویدیو و گزارش تصویری',
              },
              {
                key: 'video',
                label: 'فقط ویدیو',
                desc: 'نمایش صرفاً نوار ویدیو در صفحه بخش‌ها',
              },
              {
                key: 'photo-report',
                label: 'فقط گزارش تصویری',
                desc: 'نمایش صرفاً بخش گزارش تصویری',
              },
              {
                key: 'none',
                label: 'عدم نمایش',
                desc: 'مخفی کردن هر دو در صفحه بخش‌ها',
              },
            ] as { key: CategoryMediaMode; label: string; desc: string }[]
          ).map((opt) => {
            const currentMode: CategoryMediaMode = menuConfig.categoryMediaMode || 'both';
            const isSelected = currentMode === opt.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onChangeMenuConfig(
                    {
                      ...menuConfig,
                      categoryMediaMode: opt.key,
                    },
                    true
                  )
                }
                className={`text-right rounded-md border p-3 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-sky-50 border-[#0b5e85] text-[#0b5e85]'
                    : 'bg-white border-neutral-200 text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-50'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="font-bold flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span>{opt.label}</span>
                  {isSelected && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />}
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">{opt.desc}</p>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Add New Menu or Submenu Block to Home Page */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-neutral-100 pb-2.5">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#d41a24]" />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900">
            افزودن منوی اصلی یا زیرمنو به چینش صفحه اول
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
          {/* Source Type */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
              نوع بخش
            </label>
            <select
              value={newSourceType}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const nextType = e.target.value as HomeBlockSourceType;
                setNewSourceType(nextType);
                if (nextType === 'submenu' && selectedMenuForNew?.subsections.length) {
                  setNewSubsectionLabel(selectedMenuForNew.subsections[0]);
                }
              }}
              className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white font-semibold"
            >
              <option value="menu">منوی اصلی</option>
              <option value="submenu">زیرمنو (بخش فرعی)</option>
              <option value="article-type">بر اساس نوع مطلب</option>
              <option value="special-feed">خوراک ویژه (پربیننده‌ترین / آخرین)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Source Selection */}
          {newSourceType === 'special-feed' ? (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                انتخاب خوراک ویژه
              </label>
              <select
                value={newSpecialFeedKey}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setNewSpecialFeedKey(e.target.value as 'most-viewed' | 'latest')
                }
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="most-viewed">پربیننده‌ترین مطالب</option>
                <option value="latest">آخرین عناوین خبری</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          ) : newSourceType === 'article-type' ? (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                انتخاب نوع مطلب
              </label>
              <select
                value={newArticleTypeName}
                onChange={(e) => setNewArticleTypeName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
              >
                {activeTypes.map((t) => (
                  <option key={t.id} value={t.name}>
                    {t.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                انتخاب منوی اصلی
              </label>
              <select
                value={newSectionKey}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const key = e.target.value;
                  setNewSectionKey(key);
                  const matched = menuOptions.find((m) => m.key === key);
                  setNewSubsectionLabel(matched?.subsections[0] || '');
                }}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
              >
                {menuOptions.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>
                    {m.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Submenu Selection */}
          {newSourceType === 'submenu' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                انتخاب زیرمنو (فرعی)
              </label>
              {selectedMenuForNew && selectedMenuForNew.subsections.length > 0 ? (
                <select
                  value={newSubsectionLabel}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewSubsectionLabel(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
                >
                  {selectedMenuForNew.subsections.map((sub) => (
                    <option key={sub} value={sub}>
                      {sub}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              ) : (
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newSubsectionLabel}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewSubsectionLabel(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="نام زیرمنو را بنویسید..."
                  className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
                />
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Custom Box Title */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
              عنوان نمایشی در صفحه اول (اختیاری)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={newTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setNewTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="پیش‌فرض: نام همان منو یا زیرمنو"
              className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Zone (Where on Home Page) */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
              محل نمایش در صفحه اول (کجا)
            </label>
            <select
              value={newZone}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const z = e.target.value as HomeBlockZone;
                setNewZone(z);
                if (z === 'sidebar') {
                  setNewLayout('headline-list');
                } else if (z === 'full-top' || z === 'full-bottom') {
                  setNewLayout('grid-4col');
                }
              }}
              className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
            >
              <option value="full-top">نوار تمام‌عرض بالا (زیر سرخط)</option>
              <option value="main">ستون اصلی صفحه (سمت راست — عریض)</option>
              <option value="sidebar">ستون کناری صفحه (سمت چپ — سایدبار)</option>
              <option value="full-bottom">نوار تمام‌عرض پایین صفحه</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Width in Main Zone */}
          {newZone === 'main' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
                پهنا در ستون اصلی
              </label>
              <select
                value={newColSpan}
                onChange={(e) => setNewColSpan(e.target.value as 'full' | 'half')}
                className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="full">تمام‌عرض ستون اصلی (تکی)</option>
                <option value="half">نیم‌عرض (دوتایی کنار باکس بعدی)</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Layout / Shape */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
              شکل و قالب نمایش (چگونه)
            </label>
            <select
              value={newLayout}
              onChange={(e) => setNewLayout(e.target.value as HomeBlockLayout)}
              className="w-full rounded border border-neutral-300 px-2.5 py-2 bg-white"
            >
              {LAYOUT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.key} value={opt.key}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 mb-1">
              تعداد مطلب قابل نمایش
            </label>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setNewArticleCount((c) => Math.max(1, c - 1))}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded border border-neutral-300 bg-white hover:bg-neutral-50 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}

                className="flex-1 text-center rounded border border-neutral-300 py-1.5 bg-white font-mono"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setNewArticleCount((c) => Math.min(30, c + 1))}

                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />

import { Camera, ChevronLeft, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  Article,
  DEFAULT_HERO_CONFIG,
  DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS,
  HomeLayoutBlock,
  MenuConfig,
  SectionKey,
  toPersianDigits,
} from '../types/news';
import { SafeImage } from './SafeImage';
import { SectionHeader } from './SectionHeader';

interface HomeViewProps {
  articles: Article[];
  menuConfig: MenuConfig;
  onSelectArticle: (article: Article) => void;
  onSelectSection: (section: SectionKey, subsection?: string | null) => void;
  onSelectArticleType?: (articleType: string) => void;
  onPlayVideoArticle: (article: Article) => void;
}

function CompactHorizontalCard({
  article,
  onClick,
  numberBadge,
}: {
  article: Article;
  onClick: () => void;
  numberBadge?: number;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className="w-full flex items-start gap-3 text-right group cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#0b5e85] rounded-sm pb-3 border-b border-neutral-100 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0 sm:p-1 sm:-m-1 sm:border-b-0 transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-50"
    >
      <div className="relative w-28 h-[72px] sm:w-24 sm:h-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-neutral-100">
        <SafeImage
          src={article.imageUrl}
          alt={article.title}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
        />
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5">
          {numberBadge !== undefined && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 rounded-[3px] bg-[#d41a24] text-white text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0 mt-1 sm:mt-0.5 tabular-nums">
              {toPersianDigits(numberBadge)}
            </span>
          )}
          <h3 className="text-[13px] sm:text-xs leading-6 sm:leading-5 font-semibold text-neutral-800 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] transition-colors line-clamp-3 sm:line-clamp-2">
            {article.title}
          </h3>
        </div>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}

function dedupeArticles(list: Article[]): Article[] {
  const seen = new Set<number>();
  const out: Article[] = [];
  for (const item of list) {
    if (!seen.has(item.id)) {
      seen.add(item.id);
      out.push(item);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function resolveBlockArticles(
  block: HomeLayoutBlock,
  articles: Article[],
  nonHero: Article[]
): Article[] {
  const count = Math.max(1, Math.min(30, block.articleCount || 4));

  if (block.sourceType === 'special-feed') {
    if (block.sectionKey === 'most-viewed') {
      return [...articles].sort((a, b) => b.views - a.views).slice(0, count);
    }
    return articles.slice(0, count);
  }

  if (block.sourceType === 'submenu' && block.subsectionLabel) {
    const sub = block.subsectionLabel.trim().toLowerCase();
    const exact = articles.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.subsection.toLowerCase() === sub ||
        a.tags.toLowerCase().includes(sub) ||
        (a.section === block.sectionKey &&
          (a.title.toLowerCase().includes(sub) || a.lead.toLowerCase().includes(sub)))
    );
    if (block.strictFilter) {
      return exact.slice(0, count);
    }
    const parentMatches = articles.filter((a) => a.section === block.sectionKey);
    return dedupeArticles([...exact, ...parentMatches, ...nonHero]).slice(0, count);
  }

  if (block.sourceType === 'article-type') {
    const targetType = block.sectionKey.trim();
    const exact = articles.filter((a) => {
      const aType = a.articleType || (a.isVideo ? 'ویدیو' : 'خبر');
      if (targetType === 'ویدیو') return aType === 'ویدیو' || a.isVideo;
      if (targetType === 'گفتگو') return aType === 'گفتگو' || a.section === 'interview';
      return aType === targetType;
    });
    if (block.strictFilter) {
      return exact.slice(0, count);
    }
    return dedupeArticles([...exact, ...nonHero]).slice(0, count);
  }

  // sourceType === 'menu'
  let exact: Article[];
  if (block.sectionKey === 'world') {
    exact = articles.filter((a) => a.section === 'world' || a.section === 'africa');
  } else if (block.sectionKey === 'home') {
    exact = articles;
  } else {
    exact = articles.filter((a) => a.section === block.sectionKey);
  }

  if (block.strictFilter) {
    return exact.slice(0, count);
  }
  return dedupeArticles([...exact, ...nonHero]).slice(0, count);
}

function renderBlockBody(
  block: HomeLayoutBlock,
  items: Article[],
  inSidebar: boolean,
  onSelectArticle: (article: Article) => void,
  onPlayVideoArticle: (article: Article) => void
) {
  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="py-6 px-4 text-center border border-dashed border-neutral-200 rounded bg-neutral-50 text-xs text-neutral-500">
        هنوز مطلبی برای «{block.title}» ثبت نشده است.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isVideoItem = (item: Article) =>
    item.isVideo || item.articleType === 'ویدیو' || item.articleType === 'ویدئو';
  const isVideoBlock =
    block.sourceType === 'article-type' &&
    (block.sectionKey === 'ویدیو' || block.sectionKey === 'ویدئو');
  const handleItemClick = (item: Article) => {
    if ((isVideoBlock || block.layout === 'media-dark') && isVideoItem(item)) {
      onPlayVideoArticle(item);
    } else {
      onSelectArticle(item);
    }
  };

  switch (block.layout) {
    case 'featured-split': {
      const mainItem = items[0];
      const sideItems = items.slice(1);
      return (
        <div
          className={`grid grid-cols-1 ${
            inSidebar ? 'gap-4' : 'md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6'
          } items-start`}
        >
          <div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(mainItem)}
              className="w-full text-right group cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="w-full aspect-[16/10] overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-neutral-100 mb-2.5">
                <SafeImage
                  src={mainItem.imageUrl}
                  alt={mainItem.title}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.03] transition-transform duration-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 mb-1.5">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#d41a24] shrink-0 mt-2.5 sm:mt-2" />
                <h3 className="text-[15px] sm:text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] leading-7 sm:leading-6">
                  {mainItem.title}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[13px] sm:text-xs leading-6 sm:leading-5 text-neutral-600 line-clamp-3">
                {mainItem.lead}
              </p>
            </button>
          </div>

          {sideItems.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-3.5 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-100 md:pt-0 md:border-t-0">
              {sideItems.map((item, idx) => (
                <CompactHorizontalCard
                  key={`${block.id}-fs-${item.id}-${idx}`}
                  article={item}
                  numberBadge={block.showNumbers ? idx + 2 : undefined}
                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'lead-with-list': {
      const leadItem = items[0];
      const rest = items.slice(1);
      return (
        <div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleItemClick(leadItem)}
            className="w-full text-right group cursor-pointer mb-3.5 sm:mb-4 pb-3 border-b border-neutral-100 sm:pb-0 sm:border-b-0 block"
          >
            <div className="w-full aspect-[16/10] overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-neutral-100 mb-2">
              <SafeImage
                src={leadItem.imageUrl}
                alt={leadItem.title}
                className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.03] transition-transform duration-200"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 mb-1">
              <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#d41a24] shrink-0 mt-2.5 sm:mt-2" />
              <h3 className="text-[15px] sm:text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] leading-7 sm:leading-6">
                {leadItem.title}
              </h3>
            </div>
            <p
              className={`text-[13px] sm:text-xs leading-6 sm:leading-5 text-neutral-600 line-clamp-2 ${
                inSidebar ? 'block' : 'block sm:hidden'
              }`}
            >
              {leadItem.lead}
            </p>
          </button>

          {rest.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-3.5">
              {rest.map((item, idx) => (
                <CompactHorizontalCard
                  key={`${block.id}-lwl-${item.id}-${idx}`}
                  article={item}
                  numberBadge={block.showNumbers ? idx + 2 : undefined}
                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'grid-2col': {
      return (
        <div
          className={`grid grid-cols-1 ${
            inSidebar ? 'gap-3 sm:gap-3.5' : 'gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-x-6 md:gap-y-4'
          }`}
        >
          {items.map((item, index) => (
            <CompactHorizontalCard
              key={`${block.id}-g2-${item.id}-${index}`}
              article={item}
              numberBadge={block.showNumbers ? index + 1 : undefined}
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'grid-4col': {
      return (
        <div
          className={`grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 ${
            inSidebar ? 'lg:grid-cols-1' : 'lg:grid-cols-4'
          } gap-4 sm:gap-5`}
        >
          {items.map((item, idx) => (
            <button
              key={`${block.id}-g4-${item.id}-${idx}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
              className="text-right group cursor-pointer pb-3.5 border-b border-neutral-100 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0 sm:border-b-0 sm:pb-0"
            >
              <div className="relative aspect-[16/10] rounded-sm overflow-hidden bg-neutral-100 mb-2">
                <SafeImage
                  src={item.imageUrl}
                  alt={item.title}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5">
                {block.showNumbers ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 rounded-[3px] bg-[#d41a24] text-white text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0 mt-1 sm:mt-0.5 tabular-nums">
                    {toPersianDigits(idx + 1)}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#d41a24] shrink-0 mt-2.5 sm:mt-2" />
                )}
                <h3 className="text-[14px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] leading-6 sm:leading-5 line-clamp-2">
                  {item.title}
                </h3>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'list-compact': {
      return (
        <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-3.5">
          {items.map((item, idx) => (
            <CompactHorizontalCard
              key={`${block.id}-lc-${item.id}-${idx}`}
              article={item}
              numberBadge={block.showNumbers ? idx + 1 : undefined}
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'headline-list': {
      return (
        <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-200 border-b border-neutral-200">
          {items.map((item, idx) => (
            <button
              key={`${block.id}-hl-${item.id}-${idx}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
              className="w-full py-3 sm:py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 text-right group cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                {block.showNumbers && (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 rounded-[3px] bg-[#d41a24] text-white text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0 mt-1 sm:mt-0 tabular-nums">
                    {toPersianDigits(idx + 1)}
                  </span>
                )}
                <span className="text-[13px] sm:text-xs leading-6 sm:leading-5 text-neutral-800 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] transition-colors line-clamp-2 sm:line-clamp-1 font-medium">
                  {item.title}
                </span>
              </div>
              <span className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4 rounded-full bg-neutral-200 group-hover:bg-[#d41a24] text-neutral-600 group-hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-colors">
                <ChevronLeft className="w-3 h-3" />
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'avatar-list': {
      return (
        <div className="space-y-3 sm:space-y-3.5">
          {items.map((item, idx) => (
            <button
              key={`${block.id}-av-${item.id}-${idx}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 text-right group cursor-pointer pb-3 border-b border-neutral-100 last:border-b-0 last:pb-0 sm:border-b-0 sm:pb-0"
            >
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full overflow-hidden shrink-0 bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200">
                <SafeImage
                  src={item.authorAvatar || item.imageUrl}
                  alt={item.title}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-[13.5px] sm:text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] leading-6 sm:leading-5 line-clamp-2 transition-colors">
                  {item.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 mt-1 tabular-nums">
                  {item.publishedLabel.split(' ساعت')[0] || '۱۴ فروردین ۱۴۰۵'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      );
    }

    case 'media-dark': {
      const mediaMain = items[0];
      const mediaSide = items.slice(1, 4);
      const extraMedia = items.slice(4);

      if (inSidebar) {
        return (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {items.map((vid, idx) => {
              const itemIsVideo = isVideoItem(vid);
              return (
                <button
                  key={`${block.id}-mside-${vid.id}-${idx}`}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(vid)}
                  className="relative w-full aspect-[16/9] rounded-sm overflow-hidden group cursor-pointer block bg-black"
                >
                  <SafeImage
                    src={vid.imageUrl}
                    alt={vid.title}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-85 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                  />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-white/80 group-hover:bg-white text-neutral-900 flex items-center justify-center shadow">
                      {itemIsVideo ? (
                        <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current mr-[-2px]" />
                      ) : (
                        <Camera className="w-4 h-4" />
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="absolute bottom-2 inset-x-3 text-right">
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-[11px] font-semibold text-white line-clamp-2 sm:line-clamp-1">
                      {vid.title}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        );
      }

      const mainIsVideo = isVideoItem(mediaMain);

      return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-3.5 sm:gap-4 items-stretch">
            <div className={mediaSide.length > 0 ? 'lg:col-span-8' : 'lg:col-span-12'}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleItemClick(mediaMain)}
                className="relative w-full h-full min-h-[210px] sm:min-h-[260px] md:min-h-[360px] rounded-sm overflow-hidden group cursor-pointer block text-right bg-black"
              >
                <SafeImage
                  src={mediaMain.imageUrl}
                  alt={mediaMain.title}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-90 group-hover:scale-[1.02] transition-transform duration-300"
                />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/85 via-black/25 to-transparent" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <span className="w-14 h-14 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 rounded-full bg-white/80 group-hover:bg-white text-neutral-900 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg transition-transform group-hover:scale-105">
                    {mainIsVideo ? (
                      <Play className="w-6 h-6 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 fill-current mr-[-2px]" />
                    ) : (
                      <Camera className="w-6 h-6 sm:w-7 sm:h-7" />
                    )}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 p-3.5 sm:p-4 md:p-5 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-sm sm:text-base md:text-lg font-bold text-white leading-6 line-clamp-2 sm:line-clamp-1">
                    {mediaMain.title}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </button>
            </div>

            {mediaSide.length > 0 && (
              <div className="lg:col-span-4 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:flex lg:flex-col gap-3 justify-between">
                {mediaSide.map((vid, idx) => {
                  const sideIsVideo = isVideoItem(vid);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={`${block.id}-ms-${vid.id}-${idx}`}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleItemClick(vid)}
                      className="relative w-full flex-1 min-h-[140px] sm:min-h-[120px] lg:min-h-[108px] rounded-sm overflow-hidden group cursor-pointer block bg-black"
                    >
                      <SafeImage
                        src={vid.imageUrl}
                        alt={vid.title}
                        className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-85 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                      />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/75 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-white/80 group-hover:bg-white text-neutral-900 flex items-center justify-center shadow transition-transform group-hover:scale-105">
                          {sideIsVideo ? (
                            <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current mr-[-2px]" />
                          ) : (
                            <Camera className="w-4 h-4" />
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="absolute bottom-2 inset-x-3 text-right">
                        <p className="text-xs sm:text-[11px] font-semibold text-white line-clamp-2 sm:line-clamp-1">
                          {vid.title}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {extraMedia.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {extraMedia.map((vid, idx) => {
                const extraIsVideo = isVideoItem(vid);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={`${block.id}-mextra-${vid.id}-${idx}`}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleItemClick(vid)}
                    className="relative w-full aspect-[16/10] rounded-sm overflow-hidden group cursor-pointer block bg-black"
                  >
                    <SafeImage
                      src={vid.imageUrl}
                      alt={vid.title}
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-85 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                    />
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-white/80 group-hover:bg-white text-neutral-900 flex items-center justify-center shadow">
                        {extraIsVideo ? (
                          <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current mr-[-2px]" />
                        ) : (
                          <Camera className="w-4 h-4" />
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute bottom-2 inset-x-3 text-right">
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-white line-clamp-1">
                        {vid.title}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }
  }
}

export function HomeView({
  articles,
  menuConfig,
  onSelectArticle,
  onSelectSection,
  onSelectArticleType,
  onPlayVideoArticle,
}: HomeViewProps) {
  if (articles.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-4 py-16 text-center text-neutral-500 text-sm">
        خبری برای نمایش یافت نشد.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const heroCfg = menuConfig.heroConfig || DEFAULT_HERO_CONFIG;
  const blocks = (menuConfig.homeBlocks && menuConfig.homeBlocks.length > 0
    ? menuConfig.homeBlocks
    : DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS
  ).filter((b) => b.enabled);

  // Hero pool
  let heroPool = articles;
  if (heroCfg.sourceType === 'menu' && heroCfg.sectionKey !== 'home') {
    const matched = articles.filter((a) => a.section === heroCfg.sectionKey);
    if (matched.length > 0) heroPool = dedupeArticles([...matched, ...articles]);
  } else if (heroCfg.sourceType === 'submenu' && heroCfg.subsectionLabel) {
    const sub = heroCfg.subsectionLabel.toLowerCase();
    const matched = articles.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.subsection.toLowerCase() === sub ||
        a.tags.toLowerCase().includes(sub) ||
        a.title.toLowerCase().includes(sub)
    );
    if (matched.length > 0) heroPool = dedupeArticles([...matched, ...articles]);
  }

  const heroMain = heroPool.find((a) => a.isHeroMain) || heroPool[0];
  const nonHero = articles.filter((a) => a.id !== heroMain.id);
  const heroPoolNonHero = heroPool.filter((a) => a.id !== heroMain.id);

  const rightCount = Math.max(0, Math.min(12, heroCfg.sideRightCount ?? 6));
  const leftCount = Math.max(0, Math.min(12, heroCfg.sideLeftCount ?? 6));
  const heroRight = heroPoolNonHero.slice(0, rightCount);
  const heroLeft = heroPoolNonHero.slice(rightCount, rightCount + leftCount);

  const fullTopBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.zone === 'full-top');
  const mainBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.zone === 'main');
  const sidebarBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.zone === 'sidebar');
  const fullBottomBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.zone === 'full-bottom');

  const handleMore = (block: HomeLayoutBlock) => {
    if (block.sourceType === 'special-feed') {
      onSelectSection('asia', null);
      return;
    }
    if (block.sourceType === 'article-type') {
      if (onSelectArticleType) {
        onSelectArticleType(block.sectionKey);
      } else if (block.sectionKey === 'گفتگو') {
        onSelectSection('interview', null);
      } else {
        onSelectSection('west-asia', null);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (block.sourceType === 'submenu' && block.subsectionLabel) {
      onSelectSection(block.sectionKey, block.subsectionLabel);
      return;
    }
    onSelectSection(block.sectionKey || 'home', null);
  };

  // Group main column blocks into full-width and half-width pairs
  type MainRow =
    | { type: 'full'; block: HomeLayoutBlock }
    | { type: 'pair'; blocks: HomeLayoutBlock[] };

  const mainRows: MainRow[] = [];
  for (const b of mainBlocks) {
    if (b.columnSpan === 'half') {
      const last = mainRows[mainRows.length - 1];
      if (last && last.type === 'pair' && last.blocks.length < 2) {
        last.blocks.push(b);
      } else {
        mainRows.push({ type: 'pair', blocks: [b] });
      }
    } else {
      mainRows.push({ type: 'full', block: b });
    }
  }

  const renderFullWidthBand = (block: HomeLayoutBlock) => {
    const items = resolveBlockArticles(block, articles, nonHero);
    const isDark = block.layout === 'media-dark';
    return (
      <section
        key={block.id}
        className={`w-full py-6 sm:py-8 ${
          isDark ? 'bg-[#242424] text-white' : 'bg-white text-neutral-900 border-t border-neutral-100'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-3.5 sm:px-4">
          <SectionHeader
            title={block.title}
            subtitle={block.sourceType === 'submenu' && block.subsectionLabel && block.subsectionLabel !== block.title ? block.subsectionLabel : undefined}
            dark={isDark}
            onMore={() => handleMore(block)}
          />
          {renderBlockBody(block, items, false, onSelectArticle, onPlayVideoArticle)}
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  };

  const renderColumnBlock = (block: HomeLayoutBlock, inSidebar: boolean) => {
    const items = resolveBlockArticles(block, articles, nonHero);
    const isDark = block.layout === 'media-dark';
    return (
      <div
        key={block.id}
        className={isDark ? 'bg-[#242424] text-white p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-sm' : ''}
      >
        <SectionHeader
          title={block.title}
          subtitle={block.sourceType === 'submenu' && block.subsectionLabel && block.subsectionLabel !== block.title ? block.subsectionLabel : undefined}
          dark={isDark}
          onMore={() => handleMore(block)}
        />
        {renderBlockBody(block, items, inSidebar, onSelectArticle, onPlayVideoArticle)}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <main className="w-full">
      {/* 1. Top 3-Column Hero Grid */}
      {heroCfg.enabled && (
        <section className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-3.5 sm:px-4 pt-4 sm:pt-8 pb-6 sm:pb-8">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-5 sm:gap-6 items-start">
            {/* Right Column */}
            {rightCount > 0 && (
              <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-3 sm:space-y-0 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-2 sm:gap-4 lg:block lg:space-y-4 order-2 lg:order-1 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-100 lg:pt-0 lg:border-t-0">
                {heroRight.map((item, idx) => (
                  <CompactHorizontalCard
                    key={`hero-right-${item.id}-${idx}`}
                    article={item}
                    onClick={() => onSelectArticle(item)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Center Column: Dominant Lead Story */}
            <div
              className={`order-1 lg:order-2 ${
                rightCount > 0 && leftCount > 0
                  ? 'lg:col-span-6'
                  : rightCount > 0 || leftCount > 0
                    ? 'lg:col-span-9'
                    : 'lg:col-span-12'
              }`}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectArticle(heroMain)}
                className="w-full text-right group cursor-pointer focus:outline-none"
              >
                <div className="w-full aspect-[16/10] overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-neutral-100 mb-3">
                  <SafeImage
                    src={heroMain.imageUrl}
                    alt={heroMain.title}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.02] transition-transform duration-300"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 mb-2">
                  <span
                    className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#d41a24] shrink-0 mt-3 sm:mt-2.5"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                  <h1 className="text-[18px] sm:text-lg md:text-xl font-extrabold text-neutral-900 group-hover:text-[#d41a24] transition-colors leading-8 sm:leading-snug">
                    {heroMain.title}
                  </h1>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[13.5px] sm:text-[13px] leading-7 sm:leading-6 text-neutral-700 sm:text-neutral-600 line-clamp-3">
                  {heroMain.lead}
                </p>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Left Column */}
            {leftCount > 0 && (
              <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-3 sm:space-y-0 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-2 sm:gap-4 lg:block lg:space-y-4 order-3 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-100 lg:pt-0 lg:border-t-0">
                {heroLeft.map((item, idx) => (
                  <CompactHorizontalCard
                    key={`hero-left-${item.id}-${idx}`}
                    article={item}
                    onClick={() => onSelectArticle(item)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* 2. Full-Width Top Bands */}
      {fullTopBlocks.map(renderFullWidthBand)}

      {/* 3. Main Content + Left Sidebar */}
      {(mainRows.length > 0 || sidebarBlocks.length > 0) && (
        <section className="max-w-[1140px] mx-auto px-3.5 sm:px-4 pt-5 sm:pt-8 pb-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-7 lg:gap-8 items-start">
            {/* Right Main Column (8 cols, or 12 if sidebar is empty) */}
            {mainRows.length > 0 && (
              <div
                className={`${
                  sidebarBlocks.length > 0 ? 'lg:col-span-8' : 'lg:col-span-12'
                } space-y-7 sm:space-y-10`}
              >
                {mainRows.map((row, rIdx) =>
                  row.type === 'full' ? (
                    renderColumnBlock(row.block, false)
                  ) : (
                    <div
                      key={`pair-${row.blocks.map((b) => b.id).join('-')}-${rIdx}`}
                      className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-7 md:gap-6"
                    >
                      {row.blocks.map((b) => renderColumnBlock(b, false))}
                    </div>
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Left Sidebar (4 cols, or 12 if main is empty) */}
            {sidebarBlocks.length > 0 && (
              <aside
                className={`${
                  mainRows.length > 0 ? 'lg:col-span-4' : 'lg:col-span-12'
                } space-y-7 sm:space-y-10 pt-5 border-t border-neutral-200 lg:pt-0 lg:border-t-0`}
              >
                {sidebarBlocks.map((block) => renderColumnBlock(block, true))}
              </aside>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* 4. Full-Width Bottom Bands */}
      {fullBottomBlocks.map(renderFullWidthBand)}
    </main>
  );
}

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\components\SafeImage.tsx === 
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Newspaper } from 'lucide-react';

interface SafeImageProps {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  className?: string;
}

export function SafeImage({ src, alt, className = '' }: SafeImageProps) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  if (failed || !src) {
    return (
      <div
        className={`flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-200 via-sky-50 to-slate-300 text-slate-500 ${className}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={alt}
      >
        <Newspaper className="w-6 h-6 opacity-60" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <img
      src={src}
      alt={alt}
      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
      className={className}
    />
  );
}

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\context\AdminAuthContext.tsx === 
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState } from 'react';

interface AdminAuthContextValue {
  isAdmin: boolean;
  adminToken: string | null;
  loginError: string | null;
  adminLogin: (user: string, pass: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  adminLogout: () => void;
  getAdminToken: () => string | null;
}

const AdminAuthContext = createContext<AdminAuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AdminAuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [adminToken, setAdminToken] = useState<string | null>(
    localStorage.getItem('islam_admin_token')
  );
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState<boolean>(
    Boolean(localStorage.getItem('islam_admin_token'))
  );
  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const adminLogin = async (user: string, pass: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    setLoginError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username: user, password: pass }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok || !data.token) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است.');
      }
      setAdminToken(data.token);
      setIsAdmin(true);
      localStorage.setItem('islam_admin_token', data.token);
      return true;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در ورود به پنل مدیریت';
      setLoginError(msg);
      return false;
    }
  };

  const adminLogout = () => {
    setAdminToken(null);
    setIsAdmin(false);
    localStorage.removeItem('islam_admin_token');
  };

  const getAdminToken = () => {
    return adminToken || localStorage.getItem('islam_admin_token');
  };

  return (
    <AdminAuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        isAdmin,
        adminToken,
        loginError,
        adminLogin,
        adminLogout,
        getAdminToken,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AdminAuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAdminAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AdminAuthContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error('useAdminAuth must be used inside AdminAuthProvider');
  }
  return ctx;
}

import { defineConfig } from 'drizzle-kit';
import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';

dotenv.config();

const sqlHost = process.env.SQL_HOST;
const sqlDbName = process.env.SQL_DB_NAME;
const user = process.env.SQL_ADMIN_USER;
const password = process.env.SQL_ADMIN_PASSWORD;

if (!sqlHost) {
  throw new Error('SQL_HOST must be set in environment variables.');
}
if (!sqlDbName) {
  throw new Error('SQL_DB_NAME must be set in environment variables.');
}
if (!user) {
  throw new Error('SQL_ADMIN_USER must be set in environment variables.');
}
if (!password) {
  throw new Error('SQL_ADMIN_PASSWORD must be set in environment variables.');
}

export default defineConfig({
  schema: './src/db/schema.ts',
  out: './drizzle',
  dialect: 'postgresql',
  schemaFilter: ['public'],
  dbCredentials: {
    host: sqlHost,
    user: user,
    password: password,
    database: sqlDbName,
    ssl: false,
  },
  verbose: true,

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\db\index.ts === 
import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres';
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import * as schema from './schema.ts';

declare global {
  var _postgresPool: Pool | undefined;
}

export const createPool = () => {
  if (!global._postgresPool) {
    global._postgresPool = new Pool({
      host: process.env.SQL_HOST,
      user: process.env.SQL_USER,
      password: process.env.SQL_PASSWORD,
      database: process.env.SQL_DB_NAME,
      max: 10,
      connectionTimeoutMillis: 15000,
    });

    global._postgresPool.on('error', (err) => {
      console.error('Unexpected error on idle SQL pool client:', err);
    });
  }
  return global._postgresPool;
};

const pool = createPool();

export const db = drizzle(pool, { schema });

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\db\newsRepository.ts === 
import { desc, eq, ilike, or, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { db } from './index.ts';
import {
  articles,
  comments,
  newsletterSubscribers,
  siteSettings,
  videoAnimations,
} from './schema.ts';
import { ensureSeeded } from './seed.ts';

export interface ArticleFilters {
  section?: string;
  search?: string;
  language?: string;
}

export async function listArticles(filters: ArticleFilters = {}) {
  try {
    await ensureSeeded();

    const lang = filters.language || 'fa';
    const all = await db
      .select()
      .from(articles)
      .where(eq(articles.language, lang))
      .orderBy(desc(articles.id));

    return all.filter((item) => {
      if (filters.section && filters.section !== 'home' && filters.section !== 'all') {
        if (item.section !== filters.section) {
          return false;
        }
      }
      if (filters.search && filters.search.trim().length > 0) {
        const q = filters.search.trim().toLowerCase();
        const hay = `${item.title} ${item.lead} ${item.body} ${item.subsection} ${item.tags}`.toLowerCase();
        if (!hay.includes(q)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database query failed in listArticles:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در دریافت فهرست اخبار از پایگاه داده.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function getArticleById(id: number) {
  try {
    await ensureSeeded();
    const rows = await db.select().from(articles).where(eq(articles.id, id));
    if (!rows.length) return null;

    await db
      .update(articles)
      .set({ views: sql`${articles.views} + 1` })
      .where(eq(articles.id, id));

    const updated = await db.select().from(articles).where(eq(articles.id, id));
    return updated[0] || rows[0];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database query failed in getArticleById:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در دریافت جزئیات خبر.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function createArticle(input: {
  section: string;
  subsection: string;
  title: string;
  lead: string;
  body: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatar: string;
  sourceName?: string;
  articleType?: string;
  tags: string[];
  attachments?: Array<{ id: string; name: string; url: string; size?: number; mimeType?: string }>;
  galleryImages?: Array<{ id: string; url: string; caption?: string }>;
  videos?: Array<{ id: string; url: string; title?: string; mimeType?: string }>;
  isVideo?: boolean;
}) {
  try {
    const code = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 899999));
    const nowLabel = 'پنجشنبه ۱۴ فروردین ۱۴۰۵ ساعت ۲۲:۳۰';
    const resolvedType = input.articleType?.trim() || 'خبر';
    const cleanAttachments = Array.isArray(input.attachments) ? input.attachments : [];
    const cleanGallery = Array.isArray(input.galleryImages) ? input.galleryImages : [];
    const cleanVideos = Array.isArray(input.videos) ? input.videos : [];

    const inserted = await db
      .insert(articles)
      .values({
        code,
        language: 'fa',
        section: input.section,
        subsection: input.subsection,
        title: input.title,
        lead: input.lead,
        body: input.body,
        imageUrl: input.imageUrl,
        authorName: input.authorName,
        authorAvatar: input.authorAvatar,
        sourceName: input.sourceName || 'اسلام تایمز',
        articleType: resolvedType,
        publishedLabel: nowLabel,
        tags: JSON.stringify(input.tags),
        attachments: JSON.stringify(cleanAttachments),
        galleryImages: JSON.stringify(cleanGallery),
        videos: JSON.stringify(cleanVideos),
        isFeatured: true,
        isHeroMain: false,
        isVideo:
          input.isVideo !== undefined
            ? Boolean(input.isVideo)
            : resolvedType === 'ویدیو' || cleanVideos.length > 0,
        views: 1,
        likes: 0,
      })
      .returning();

    return inserted[0];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database insert failed in createArticle:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در انتشار خبر جدید.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function likeArticle(id: number) {
  try {
    const updated = await db
      .update(articles)
      .set({ likes: sql`${articles.likes} + 1` })
      .where(eq(articles.id, id))
      .returning();
    return updated[0] || null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database update failed in likeArticle:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در ثبت واکنش به خبر.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function createComment(input: {
  articleId: number;
  userId: number;
  parentId?: number | null;
  authorName: string;
  authorEmail: string;
  showEmail: boolean;
  content: string;
}) {
