      .insert(comments)
      .values({
        articleId: input.articleId,
        userId: input.userId,
        parentId: input.parentId ?? null,
        authorName: input.authorName,
        authorEmail: input.authorEmail,
        showEmail: input.showEmail,
        content: input.content,
        likes: 0,
        dislikes: 0,
        publishedLabel: 'پنجشنبه ۱۴ فروردین ۱۴۰۵',
      })
      .returning();

    return inserted[0];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database insert failed in createComment:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در ثبت نظر در پایگاه داده.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function voteComment(commentId: number, type: 'like' | 'dislike') {
  try {
    const updated = await db
      .update(comments)
      .set(
        type === 'like'
          ? { likes: sql`${comments.likes} + 1` }
          : { dislikes: sql`${comments.dislikes} + 1` }
      )
      .where(eq(comments.id, commentId))
      .returning();
    return updated[0] || null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database update failed in voteComment:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در ثبت رأی نظر.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function subscribeNewsletter(email: string, language = 'fa') {
  try {
    const inserted = await db
      .insert(newsletterSubscribers)
      .values({ email, language })
      .onConflictDoNothing()
      .returning();
    return inserted[0] || { email, language, alreadySubscribed: true };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database insert failed in subscribeNewsletter:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در ثبت ایمیل خبرنامه.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function searchArticlesSql(query: string) {
  try {
    await ensureSeeded();
    const q = `%${query.trim()}%`;
    return await db
      .select()
      .from(articles)
      .where(
        or(
          ilike(articles.title, q),
          ilike(articles.lead, q),
          ilike(articles.subsection, q)
        )
      )
      .orderBy(desc(articles.id));
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database query failed in searchArticlesSql:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در جستجوی اخبار.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function deleteArticle(id: number) {
  try {
    const deleted = await db
      .delete(articles)
      .where(eq(articles.id, id))
      .returning();
    return deleted[0] || null;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database delete failed in deleteArticle:', error);
    throw new Error('خطا در حذف خبر.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function getSettingJson<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(siteSettings)
      .where(eq(siteSettings.key, key));
    if (!rows.length) return null;
    return JSON.parse(rows[0].value) as T;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`Database query failed in getSettingJson(${key}):`, error);
    return null;
  }
}

export async function saveSettingJson<T>(key: string, data: T): Promise<T> {
  try {
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(data);
    await db
      .insert(siteSettings)
      .values({ key, value: serialized, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: siteSettings.key,
        set: { value: serialized, updatedAt: new Date() },
      });
    return data;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`Database save failed in saveSettingJson(${key}):`, error);
    throw new Error('خطا در ذخیره تنظیمات سایت در پایگاه داده.', { cause: error });
  }
}

export async function listAllComments() {
  try {
    return await db
      .select()
      .from(comments)
      .orderBy(desc(comments.id));
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database query failed in listAllComments:', error);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function renameArticleTypeInArticles(oldName: string, newName: string) {
  try {
    await db
      .update(articles)
      .set({ articleType: newName })
      .where(eq(articles.articleType, oldName));
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database update failed in renameArticleTypeInArticles:', error);
  }
}

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\db\schema.ts === 
import { relations } from 'drizzle-orm';
import {
  boolean,
  integer,
  pgTable,
  serial,
  text,
  timestamp,
} from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';

export const users = pgTable('users', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  uid: text('uid').notNull().unique(),
  email: text('email').notNull(),
  displayName: text('display_name'),
  photoUrl: text('photo_url'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const articles = pgTable('articles', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  code: text('code').notNull().unique(),
  language: text('language').notNull().default('fa'),
  section: text('section').notNull(),
  subsection: text('subsection').notNull(),
  title: text('title').notNull(),
  lead: text('lead').notNull(),
  body: text('body').notNull(),
  imageUrl: text('image_url').notNull(),
  authorName: text('author_name').notNull(),
  authorAvatar: text('author_avatar').notNull(),
  sourceName: text('source_name').notNull().default('اسلام تایمز'),
  articleType: text('article_type').notNull().default('خبر'),
  publishedLabel: text('published_label').notNull(),
  tags: text('tags').notNull().default('[]'),
  attachments: text('attachments').notNull().default('[]'),
  galleryImages: text('gallery_images').notNull().default('[]'),
  videos: text('videos').notNull().default('[]'),
  isFeatured: boolean('is_featured').notNull().default(false),
  isHeroMain: boolean('is_hero_main').notNull().default(false),
  isVideo: boolean('is_video').notNull().default(false),
  videoDuration: text('video_duration'),
  views: integer('views').notNull().default(120),
  likes: integer('likes').notNull().default(5),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const comments = pgTable('comments', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  articleId: integer('article_id')
    .references(() => articles.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' })
    .notNull(),
  userId: integer('user_id').references(() => users.id),
  parentId: integer('parent_id'),
  authorName: text('author_name').notNull(),
  authorEmail: text('author_email').notNull(),
  showEmail: boolean('show_email').notNull().default(false),
  content: text('content').notNull(),
  likes: integer('likes').notNull().default(0),
  dislikes: integer('dislikes').notNull().default(0),
  publishedLabel: text('published_label').notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const videoAnimations = pgTable('video_animations', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  userId: integer('user_id')
    .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' })
    .notNull(),
  title: text('title').notNull(),
  prompt: text('prompt').notNull(),
  aspectRatio: text('aspect_ratio').notNull().default('16:9'),
  sourceImageUrl: text('source_image_url'),
  videoData: text('video_data').notNull(),
  mimeType: text('mime_type').notNull().default('video/mp4'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const newsletterSubscribers = pgTable('newsletter_subscribers', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  email: text('email').notNull().unique(),
  language: text('language').notNull().default('fa'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const siteSettings = pgTable('site_settings', {
  id: serial('id').primaryKey(),
  key: text('key').notNull().unique(),
  value: text('value').notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at').defaultNow(),
});

export const usersRelations = relations(users, ({ many }) => ({
  comments: many(comments),
  videoAnimations: many(videoAnimations),
}));

export const articlesRelations = relations(articles, ({ many }) => ({
  comments: many(comments),
}));

export const commentsRelations = relations(comments, ({ one }) => ({
  article: one(articles, {
    fields: [comments.articleId],
    references: [articles.id],
  }),
  user: one(users, {
    fields: [comments.userId],
    references: [users.id],
  }),
}));

export const videoAnimationsRelations = relations(videoAnimations, ({ one }) => ({
  user: one(users, {
    fields: [videoAnimations.userId],
    references: [users.id],
  }),
}));

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\db\users.ts === 
import { db } from './index.ts';
import { users } from './schema.ts';

export async function getOrCreateUser(
  uid: string,
  email: string,
  displayName?: string | null,
  photoUrl?: string | null
) {
  try {
    const result = await db
      .insert(users)
      .values({
        uid,
        email,
        displayName: displayName || null,
        photoUrl: photoUrl || null,
      })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: users.uid,
        set: {
          email,
          displayName: displayName || null,
          photoUrl: photoUrl || null,
        },
      })
      .returning();

    return result[0];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Database user upsert failed:', error);
    throw new Error('Failed to synchronize user account.', { cause: error });
  }
}

=== C:\Users\Hooshmand\Desktop\islamtimes\src\middleware\auth.ts === 
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { DecodedIdToken } from 'firebase-admin/auth';
import { adminAuth } from '../lib/firebase-admin.ts';

export interface AuthRequest extends Request {
  user?: DecodedIdToken;
}

export const requireAuth = async (
  req: AuthRequest,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
) => {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'لطفاً ابتدا وارد حساب کاربری خود شوید.' });
  }

  const token = authHeader.split('Bearer ')[1];
  try {
    const decodedToken = await adminAuth.verifyIdToken(token);
    req.user = decodedToken;
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error verifying Firebase ID token:', error);
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'نشست کاربری نامعتبر است. لطفاً دوباره وارد شوید.' });
  }
};

export interface ArticleAttachment {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  url: string;
  size?: number;
  mimeType?: string;
}

export interface ArticleGalleryItem {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  caption?: string;
}

export interface ArticleVideoItem {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  title?: string;
  mimeType?: string;
}

export interface Article {
  id: number;
  code: string;
  language: string;
  section: string;
  subsection: string;
  title: string;
  lead: string;
  body: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorAvatar: string;
  sourceName: string;
  articleType?: string;
  publishedLabel: string;
  tags: string;
  attachments?: string;
  galleryImages?: string;
  videos?: string;
  isFeatured: boolean;
  isHeroMain: boolean;
  isVideo: boolean;
  videoDuration?: string | null;
  views: number;
  likes: number;
  createdAt?: string;
}

export interface ArticleTypeOption {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  code: string;
  description: string;
  isDefault?: boolean;
}

export type HomeBlockZone = 'full-top' | 'main' | 'sidebar' | 'full-bottom';

export type HomeBlockLayout =
  | 'featured-split'
  | 'lead-with-list'
  | 'grid-2col'
  | 'grid-4col'
  | 'list-compact'
  | 'headline-list'
  | 'avatar-list'
  | 'media-dark';

export type HomeBlockSourceType = 'menu' | 'submenu' | 'article-type' | 'special-feed';

export interface HomeLayoutBlock {
  id: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  title: string;
  sourceType: HomeBlockSourceType;
  sectionKey: string;
  subsectionLabel?: string | null;
  zone: HomeBlockZone;
  columnSpan?: 'full' | 'half';
  layout: HomeBlockLayout;
  articleCount: number;
  showNumbers?: boolean;
  strictFilter?: boolean;
}

export interface HomeHeroConfig {
  enabled: boolean;
  sourceType: 'all' | 'menu' | 'submenu';
  sectionKey: string;
  subsectionLabel?: string | null;
  sideRightCount: number;
  sideLeftCount: number;
}

export interface SubmenuItemConfig {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  showOnHome: boolean;
  showOnMenuPage: boolean;
}

export interface MainMenuItemConfig {
  id: string;
  key: string;
  label: string;
  showOnHome: boolean;
  showOnMenuPage: boolean;
  homeSubmenuLimit: number;
  menuPageSubmenuLimit: number;
  subsections: SubmenuItemConfig[];
}

export type CategoryMediaMode = 'video' | 'photo-report' | 'both' | 'none';

export interface MenuConfig {
  showMainMenuOnHome: boolean;
  maxMainMenusOnHome: number;
  showSubmenusOnHome: boolean;
  maxSubmenusOnHome: number;
  showMainMenuOnMenuPage: boolean;
  maxMainMenusOnMenuPage: number;
  showSubmenusOnMenuPage: boolean;
  maxSubmenusOnMenuPage: number;
  categoryMediaMode?: CategoryMediaMode;
  items: MainMenuItemConfig[];
  heroConfig?: HomeHeroConfig;
  homeBlocks?: HomeLayoutBlock[];
}

function makeSubs(key: string, labels: string[]): SubmenuItemConfig[] {
  return labels.map((label, idx) => ({
    id: `${key}-sub-${idx + 1}`,
    label,
    showOnHome: true,
    showOnMenuPage: true,
  }));
}

export function normalizeMenuConfig(raw?: Partial<MenuConfig> | null): MenuConfig {
  if (!raw || !Array.isArray(raw.items)) {
    return DEFAULT_MENU_CONFIG;
  }

  const migratedBlocks: HomeLayoutBlock[] | undefined =
    Array.isArray(raw.homeBlocks) && raw.homeBlocks.length > 0
      ? raw.homeBlocks.map((block) => {
          if (
            block.id === 'block-media' ||
            block.sectionKey === 'media' ||
            block.title.trim() === 'مدیا'
          ) {
            return {
              ...block,
              id: block.id === 'block-media' ? 'block-video' : block.id,
              title: block.title.trim() === 'مدیا' ? 'ویدیو' : block.title,
              sourceType: 'article-type',
              sectionKey: 'ویدیو',
              subsectionLabel: null,
            };
          }
          return block;
        })
      : undefined;

  const validCategoryMediaMode: CategoryMediaMode =
    raw.categoryMediaMode === 'video' ||
    raw.categoryMediaMode === 'photo-report' ||
    raw.categoryMediaMode === 'both' ||
    raw.categoryMediaMode === 'none'
      ? raw.categoryMediaMode
      : 'both';

  return {
    ...DEFAULT_MENU_CONFIG,
    ...raw,
    categoryMediaMode: validCategoryMediaMode,
    items: raw.items,
    heroConfig: raw.heroConfig
      ? { ...DEFAULT_HERO_CONFIG, ...raw.heroConfig }
      : DEFAULT_HERO_CONFIG,
    homeBlocks: migratedBlocks || DEFAULT_HOME_BLOCKS,
  };
}

export function toPersianDigits(value: number | string): string {
  const digits = ['۰', '۱', '۲', '۳', '۴', '۵', '۶', '۷', '۸', '۹'];
  return String(value).replace(/\d/g, (d) => digits[Number(d)] ?? d);
}

export function parseTags(tagsJson: string): string[] {
  if (!tagsJson) return [];
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(tagsJson);
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      return parsed.map((t) => String(t).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
  } catch {
    return tagsJson
      .split(/[،,]/)
      .map((t) => t.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
  }
  return [];
}

export function parseJsonArray<T>(raw?: string | null): T[] {
  if (!raw) return [];
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed as T[];
  } catch {
    // ignore malformed json
  }
  return [];
}

export type AdminSidebarMenuId =
  | 'overview'
  | 'menus'
  | 'articles'
  | 'editorial'
  | 'reporters'
  | 'comments';

export interface AdminSidebarMenuItem {
  id: AdminSidebarMenuId;
  label: string;
}

export const DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS: AdminSidebarMenuItem[] = [
  { id: 'overview', label: 'داشبورد و آمار' },
  { id: 'menus', label: 'مدیریت منوها و زیرمنوها' },
  { id: 'articles', label: 'مدیریت اخبار' },
  { id: 'editorial', label: 'مدیریت تحریریه' },
  { id: 'reporters', label: 'خبرنگاران و نویسندگان' },
  { id: 'comments', label: 'مدیریت نظرات' },
];

export function normalizeAdminSidebarMenus(raw?: unknown): AdminSidebarMenuItem[] {
  const defaultMap = new Map(DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) {
    return DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS;
  }

  const seen = new Set<AdminSidebarMenuId>();
  const result: AdminSidebarMenuItem[] = [];

  for (const entry of raw) {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') continue;
    const rawId = (entry as { id?: unknown }).id as AdminSidebarMenuId;
    const def = defaultMap.get(rawId);
    if (!def || seen.has(rawId)) continue;
    seen.add(rawId);
    const rawLabel = (entry as { label?: unknown }).label;
    const label = typeof rawLabel === 'string' && rawLabel.trim() ? rawLabel.trim() : def.label;
    result.push({ id: rawId, label });
  }

  for (const def of DEFAULT_ADMIN_SIDEBAR_MENUS) {
    if (!seen.has(def.id)) {
      result.push(def);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

export interface CustomShareChannel {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  urlTemplate: string;
  color?: string;
  enabled: boolean;
}

export const DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS: CustomShareChannel[] = [
  {
    id: 'eitaa',
    name: 'ایتا (Eitaa)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://eitaa.com/share/url?url={url}&text={title}',
    color: '#d35400',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'rubika',
    name: 'روبیکا (Rubika)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://rubika.ir/share?url={url}',
    color: '#7928ca',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'bale',
    name: 'بله (Bale)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://ble.ir/share/url?url={url}&text={title}',
    color: '#10b981',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'soroush',
    name: 'سروش پلاس (Soroush+)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://splus.ir/share?url={url}&text={title}',
    color: '#0288d1',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'linkedin',
    name: 'لینکدین (LinkedIn)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://www.linkedin.com/sharing/share-offsite/?url={url}',
    color: '#0a66c2',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'pinterest',
    name: 'پینترست (Pinterest)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?url={url}&description={title}',
    color: '#bd081c',
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    id: 'reddit',
    name: 'ردیت (Reddit)',
    urlTemplate: 'https://reddit.com/submit?url={url}&title={title}',
    color: '#ff4500',
    enabled: true,
  },
];

export function normalizeShareChannels(raw?: unknown): CustomShareChannel[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw) || raw.length === 0) {
    return DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS;
  }
  const result: CustomShareChannel[] = [];
  for (const item of raw) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') continue;
    const ch = item as Partial<CustomShareChannel>;
    if (typeof ch.name === 'string' && ch.name.trim() && typeof ch.urlTemplate === 'string' && ch.urlTemplate.trim()) {
      result.push({
        id: String(ch.id || `ch-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`),
        name: ch.name.trim(),
        urlTemplate: ch.urlTemplate.trim(),
        color: typeof ch.color === 'string' && ch.color.trim() ? ch.color.trim() : '#0b5e85',
        enabled: ch.enabled !== false,
      });
    }
  }
  return result.length > 0 ? result : DEFAULT_SHARE_CHANNELS;
}
